    "                                    \"disabled\": \"{{ !(Array.isArray(_subscription_batch_v1124) && _subscription_batch_v1124.length) }}\",\n"
    "                                    \"onClick\": clear_all_js,\n"
    "                                },\n"
    "                                \"text\": \"取消全选\",\n"
    "                            }],\n"
    "                        },\n"
    "                        {\n"
    "                            \"component\": \"VCol\",\n"
    "                            \"props\": {\"cols\": 12, \"sm\": 4},\n"
    "                            \"content\": [{\n"
    "                                \"component\": \"VBtn\",\n"
    "                                \"props\": {\n"
    "                                    \"block\": True,\n"
    "                                    \"variant\": \"tonal\",\n"
    "                                    \"color\": \"primary\",\n"
    "                                    \"prepend-icon\": \"mdi-swap-horizontal\",\n"
    "                                    \"disabled\": \"{{ !(Array.isArray(_subscription_batch_v1124) && _subscription_batch_v1124.length) }}\",\n"
    "                                    \"onClick\": apply_js,\n"
    "                                },\n"
    "                                \"text\": \"添加 / 取消接管\",\n"
    "                            }],\n"
    "                        },\n"
    "                    ],\n"
    "                },\n"
    "                {\n"
    "                    \"component\": \"VAlert\",\n"
    "                    \"props\": {\n"
    "                        \"type\": \"info\",\n"
    "                        \"variant\": \"tonal\",\n"
    "                        \"density\": \"compact\",\n"
    "                        \"class\": \"mt-2\",\n"
    "                        \"text\": alert_text,\n"
    "                    },\n"
    "                },\n"
    "            ],\n"
    "        }\n"
    "\n"
    "    def get_form(self):\n"
    "        form, defaults = super().get_form()\n"
    "        defaults = dict(defaults or {})\n"
    "        defaults[\"_subscription_pick_v1124\"] = None\n"
    "        defaults[\"_subscription_batch_v1124\"] = []\n"
    "        defaults[\"_subscription_query_v1124\"] = \"\"\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            selected_node = self._find_model_node_v1124(form, \"selected_subscriptions\")\n"
    "            if selected_node is None:\n"
    "                return form, defaults\n"
    "\n"
    "            old_props = dict(selected_node.get(\"props\") or {})\n"
    "            items = list(old_props.get(\"items\") or self._subscription_options())\n"
    "            defaults[\"_subscription_catalog_v1124\"] = [\n"
    "                {\"title\": str(item.get(\"title\") or \"\"), \"value\": int(item.get(\"value\") or 0)}\n"
    "                for item in items\n"
    "                if isinstance(item, dict) and int(item.get(\"value\") or 0) > 0\n"
    "            ]\n"
    "\n"
    "            # 不再让 multiple VAutocomplete 直接绑定 selected_subscriptions。\n"
    "            # 唯一搜索态：model:search -> _subscription_query_v1124；选择态：_subscription_batch_v1124。\n"
    "            selected_node.clear()\n"
    "            selected_node.update(self._subscription_picker_card_v1124(items))\n"
    "        except Exception:\n"
    "            pass\n"
    "        return form, defaults\n"
    "\n"
    "    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n"
    "    # 数据页：快照秒开，强事实校准放后台\n"
    "    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n"
    "    @staticmethod\n"
    "    def _week_bounds_v1123() -> tuple[str, str]:\n"
    "        today = datetime.date.today()\n"
    "        start = today - datetime.timedelta(days=today.weekday())\n"
    "        end = start + datetime.timedelta(days=6)\n"
    "        return start.isoformat(), end.isoformat()\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _weekly_snapshot_usable_v1123(self, snapshot: Any) -> bool:\n"
    "        if not isinstance(snapshot, dict) or not isinstance(snapshot.get(\"days\"), list):\n"
    "            return False\n"
    "        start, end = self._week_bounds_v1123()\n"
    "        return str(snapshot.get(\"week_start\") or \"\") == start and str(snapshot.get(\"week_end\") or \"\") == end\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _weekly_snapshot_stale_v1123(self, snapshot: Dict[str, Any]) -> bool:\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            updated = datetime.datetime.fromisoformat(str(snapshot.get(\"updated_at\") or \"\"))\n"
    "        except (TypeError, ValueError):\n"
    "            return True\n"
    "        return (datetime.datetime.now() - updated).total_seconds() >= self._weekly_page_cache_seconds_v1123\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _spawn_weekly_snapshot_refresh_v1123(self) -> None:\n"
    "        lock = getattr(self, \"_weekly_page_refresh_lock_v1123\", None)\n"
    "        if lock is None:\n"
    "            lock = threading.Lock()\n"
    "            self._weekly_page_refresh_lock_v1123 = lock\n"
    "        with lock:\n"
    "            if bool(getattr(self, \"_weekly_page_refreshing_v1123\", False)):\n"
    "                return\n"
    "            self._weekly_page_refreshing_v1123 = True\n"
    "\n"
    "        def worker() -> None:\n"
    "            try:\n"
    "                # 明确跳过本层缓存方法，调用上一层真实媒体库校准快照；耗时不再阻塞页面 HTTP。\n"
    "                super(GuangYaPagePerfV1123Mixin, self)._weekly_calendar_snapshot_v1121()\n"
    "            except Exception as err:\n"
    "                try:\n"
    "                    self._plugin_log(\"WARNING\", \"【光鸭转存助手】【数据页】后台刷新追剧快照失败：%s\", type(err).__name__)\n"
    "                except Exception:\n"
    "                    pass\n"
    "            finally:\n"
    "                with lock:\n"
    "                    self._weekly_page_refreshing_v1123 = False\n"
    "\n"
    "        threading.Thread(target=worker, name=\"GuangYa-WeeklyPageRefresh\", daemon=True).start()\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _empty_week_snapshot_v1123(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        today = datetime.date.today()\n"
    "        start = today - datetime.timedelta(days=today.weekday())\n"
    "        days = []\n"
    "        for index in range(7):\n"
    "            day = start + datetime.timedelta(days=index)\n"
    "            days.append({\n"
    "                \"date\": day.isoformat(),\n"
    "                \"weekday\": index,\n"
    "                \"weekday_label\": (\"周一\", \"周二\", \"周三\", \"周四\", \"周五\", \"周六\", \"周日\")[index],\n"
    "                \"is_today\": day == today,\n"
    "                \"items\": [],\n"
    "                \"count\": 0,\n"
    "                \"library\": 0,\n"
    "                \"inflight\": 0,\n"
    "                \"pending\": 0,\n"
    "            })\n"
    "        return {\n"
    "            \"updated_at\": \"\",\n"
    "            \"week_start\": start.isoformat(),\n"
    "            \"week_end\": (start + datetime.timedelta(days=6)).isoformat(),\n"
    "            \"today\": today.isoformat(),\n"
    "            \"today_index\": today.weekday(),\n"
    "            \"week_total\": 0,\n"
    "            \"today_total\": 0,\n"
    "            \"library\": 0,\n"
    "            \"inflight\": 0,\n"
    "            \"pending\": 0,\n"
    "            \"days\": days,\n"
    "            \"movies\": [],\n"
    "            \"movie_count\": 0,\n"
    "            \"page_loading\": True,\n"
    "            \"status_source\": \"background_refresh_pending\",\n"
    "        }\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _weekly_calendar_snapshot_v1121(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        \"\"\"HTTP 页面只读最近快照；缓存过期在后台校准，避免打开数据页逐剧同步媒体库。\"\"\"\n"
    "        cached = self.get_data(\"airing_week_view_v1121\") or {}\n"
    "        if self._weekly_snapshot_usable_v1123(cached):\n"
    "            if self._weekly_snapshot_stale_v1123(cached):\n"
    "                self._spawn_weekly_snapshot_refresh_v1123()\n"
    "            result = dict(cached)\n"
    "            result[\"page_cache\"] = True\n"
    "            return result\n"
    "\n"
    "        # 第一次安装/跨周后的首次打开也先返回轻量骨架，后台立即生成真实快照。\n"
    "        self._spawn_weekly_snapshot_refresh_v1123()\n"
    "        return self._empty_week_snapshot_v1123()\n"
    "\n"
    "    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n"
    "    # 数据页：PageRender 原生即时日期切换\n"
    "    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n"
    "    def _weekly_day_rows_v1124(self, day: Dict[str, Any]) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:\n"
    "        \"\"\"生成不含海报请求的轻量剧集行，确保 7 天内容预渲染也不会卡顿。\"\"\"\n"
    "        items = [row for row in (day.get(\"items\") or []) if isinstance(row, dict)]\n"
    "        if not items:\n"
    "            return [{\n"
    "                \"component\": \"VAlert\",\n"
    "                \"props\": {\n"
    "                    \"type\": \"info\",\n"
    "                    \"variant\": \"tonal\",\n"
    "                    \"density\": \"compact\",\n"
    "                    \"text\": f\"{day.get('weekday_label') or '该日'}没有进入排期的剧集。\",\n"
    "                },\n"
    "            }]\n"
    "\n"
    "        palette = {\"library\": \"success\", \"inflight\": \"warning\", \"pending\": \"error\"}\n"
    "        rows: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []\n"
    "        for item in items:\n"
    "            status = str(item.get(\"status\") or \"pending\")\n"
    "            label = str(item.get(\"status_label\") or \"待补\")\n"
    "            title = str(item.get(\"title\") or \"未知剧集\")\n"
    "            try:\n"
    "                season = int(item.get(\"season\") or 1)\n"
    "            except (TypeError, ValueError):\n"
    "                season = 1\n"
    "            try:\n"
    "                episode = int(item.get(\"episode\") or 0)\n"
    "            except (TypeError, ValueError):\n"
    "                episode = 0\n"
    "            schedule = str(item.get(\"air_at\") or item.get(\"air_date\") or \"排期未知\")\n"
    "            rows.append({\n"
    "                \"component\": \"VCard\",\n"
    "                \"props\": {\"variant\": \"tonal\", \"class\": \"mb-2\"},\n"
    "                \"content\": [{\n"
    "                    \"component\": \"VCardText\",\n"
    "                    \"props\": {\"class\": \"py-2 px-3\"},\n"
    "                    \"content\": [\n"
    "                        {\n"
    "                            \"component\": \"div\",\n"
    "                            \"props\": {\"class\": \"d-flex align-center justify-space-between ga-2\"},\n"
    "                            \"content\": [\n"
    "                                {\"component\": \"div\", \"props\": {\"class\": \"text-body-2 font-weight-medium text-truncate flex-grow-1\"}, \"text\": title},\n"
    "                                {\"component\": \"VChip\", \"props\": {\"size\": \"x-small\", \"variant\": \"tonal\", \"color\": palette.get(status, \"error\")}, \"text\": label},\n"
    "                            ],\n"
    "                        },\n"
    "                        {\"component\": \"div\", \"props\": {\"class\": \"text-caption text-medium-emphasis mt-1\"}, \"text\": f\"S{season:02d}E{episode:02d} · {schedule}\"},\n"
    "                    ],\n"
    "                }],\n"
    "            })\n"
    "        return rows\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _weekly_page_v1121(self, snapshot: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        days = [dict(day) for day in (snapshot.get(\"days\") or []) if isinstance(day, dict)]\n"
    "        today_index = int(snapshot.get(\"today_index\") or 0)\n"
    "        # 今天始终排第一并默认展开，其余六天按顺序紧跟；点击由浏览器本地 details 原生状态处理。\n"
    "        if days and 0 <= today_index < len(days):\n"
    "            ordered_days = days[today_index:] + days[:today_index]\n"
    "        else:\n"
    "            ordered_days = days\n"
    "\n"
    "        day_details: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []\n"
    "        for day in ordered_days:\n"
    "            date_text = str(day.get(\"date\") or \"\")\n"
    "            short_date = date_text[5:].replace(\"-\", \"/\") if len(date_text) >= 10 else date_text\n"
    "            is_today = bool(day.get(\"is_today\"))\n"
    "            label = f\"今天 {short_date}\" if is_today else f\"{day.get('weekday_label') or ''} {short_date}\"\n"
    "            count = int(day.get(\"count\") or len(day.get(\"items\") or []))\n"
    "            day_details.append({\n"
    "                # PageRender 只会 v-bind props，不支持 FormRender 那套共享 v-model；\n"
    "                # 原生 details 不需要 Vue model、API 请求或 VWindow 过渡，点击即刻生效。\n"
    "                \"component\": \"details\",\n"
    "                \"props\": {\n"
    "                    \"name\": \"guangya-airing-day\",\n"
    "                    \"open\": is_today,\n"
    "                    \"class\": \"mb-2 rounded-lg border-sm\",\n"
    "                },\n"
    "                \"content\": [\n"
    "                    {\n"
    "                        \"component\": \"summary\",\n"
    "                        \"props\": {\"class\": \"px-3 py-3 cursor-pointer\"},\n"
    "                        \"content\": [{\n"
    "                            \"component\": \"div\",\n"
    "                            \"props\": {\"class\": \"d-flex flex-wrap align-center ga-2\"},\n"
    "                            \"content\": [\n"
    "                                {\"component\": \"span\", \"props\": {\"class\": \"text-body-2 font-weight-bold\"}, \"text\": f\"{label} · {count}\"},\n"
    "                                {\"component\": \"VChip\", \"props\": {\"size\": \"x-small\", \"variant\": \"tonal\", \"color\": \"success\"}, \"text\": f\"已入库 {int(day.get('library') or 0)}\"},\n"
    "                                {\"component\": \"VChip\", \"props\": {\"size\": \"x-small\", \"variant\": \"tonal\", \"color\": \"warning\"}, \"text\": f\"转存中 {int(day.get('inflight') or 0)}\"},\n"
    "                                {\"component\": \"VChip\", \"props\": {\"size\": \"x-small\", \"variant\": \"tonal\", \"color\": \"error\"}, \"text\": f\"待补 {int(day.get('pending') or 0)}\"},\n"
    "                            ],\n"
    "                        }],\n"
    "                    },\n"
    "                    {\n"
    "                        \"component\": \"div\",\n"
    "                        \"props\": {\"class\": \"px-3 pb-2\"},\n"
    "                        \"content\": self._weekly_day_rows_v1124(day),\n"
    "                    },\n"
    "                ],\n"
    "            })\n"
    "\n"
    "        loading_alert: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []\n"
    "        if snapshot.get(\"page_loading\"):\n"
    "            loading_alert.append({\n"
    "                \"component\": \"VAlert\",\n"
    "                \"props\": {\n"
    "                    \"type\": \"info\",\n"
    "                    \"variant\": \"tonal\",\n"
    "                    \"class\": \"mb-3\",\n"
    "                    \"text\": \"首次打开或刚跨周：页面先秒开，真实媒体库状态正在后台生成；稍后重新进入数据页即可看到完整排期。\",\n"
    "                },\n"
    "            })\n"
    "\n"
    "        return {\n"
    "            \"component\": \"VCard\",\n"
    "            \"props\": {\"variant\": \"outlined\", \"class\": \"mb-3\"},\n"
    "            \"content\": [\n"
    "                {\"component\": \"VCardTitle\", \"text\": \"追剧日历\"},\n"
    "                {\"component\": \"VCardSubtitle\", \"text\": \"点击任意日期立即展开对应剧集；当天默认展开。切换只发生在浏览器本地，不发接口请求、不等待后台。\"},\n"
    "                {\"component\": \"VCardText\", \"content\": [\n"
    "                    *loading_alert,\n"
    "                    {\n"
    "                        \"component\": \"div\",\n"
    "                        \"props\": {\"class\": \"d-flex flex-wrap mb-3\"},\n"
    "                        \"content\": [\n"
    "                            self._metric_chip_v1121(\"本周更新\", snapshot.get(\"week_total\") or 0, \"mdi-calendar-week\"),\n"
    "                            self._metric_chip_v1121(\"今日更新\", snapshot.get(\"today_total\") or 0, \"mdi-white-balance-sunny\"),\n"
    "                            self._metric_chip_v1121(\"已入库\", snapshot.get(\"library\") or 0, \"mdi-check-circle-outline\"),\n"
    "                            self._metric_chip_v1121(\"转存中\", snapshot.get(\"inflight\") or 0, \"mdi-progress-clock\"),\n"
    "                            self._metric_chip_v1121(\"待补\", snapshot.get(\"pending\") or 0, \"mdi-clock-alert-outline\"),\n"
    "                            self._metric_chip_v1121(\"电影待匹配\", snapshot.get(\"movie_count\") or 0, \"mdi-movie-open-outline\"),\n"
    "                        ],\n"
    "                    },\n"
    "                    *day_details,\n"
    "                    {\n"
    "                        \"component\": \"VAlert\",\n"
    "                        \"props\": {\n"
    "                            \"type\": \"info\",\n"
    "                            \"variant\": \"tonal\",\n"
    "                            \"class\": \"mt-3\",\n"
    "                            \"text\": \"星期筛选只约束普通后台追更；人工强制和每日 04:10 全员补漏仍会跨星期检查全部真实缺集。\",\n"
    "                        },\n"
    "                    },\n"
    "                ]},\n"
    "            ],\n"
    "        }\n"
    "\n"

# ===== reliability_v170.py =====
_INLINE_MODULE_SOURCES["reliability_v170"] = (
    "\"\"\"光鸭转存助手 v1.7.0 上线可靠性层。\n"
    "\n"
    "集中解决三类真实运行问题：\n"
    "1. 高频 search / API / 消息触发时的后台检查合并与同订阅最多一次补偿重查；\n"
    "2. 插件热更新/重载后旧实例仍残留事件回调或后台线程时，只允许最新实例继续工作；\n"
    "3. Telegram 镜像/频道源临时不可用时进入缓存降级 + 指数退避，并自动恢复检查。\n"
    "\"\"\"\n"
    "\n"
    "from __future__ import annotations\n"
    "\n"
    "import threading\n"
    "import time\n"
    "import weakref\n"
    "from typing import Any, Dict, Iterable, List\n"
    "\n"
    "from app.chain.subscribe import SubscribeChain\n"
    "from app.sdk.events import Event, eventmanager\n"
    "from app.schemas.types import EventType\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "class GuangYaReliabilityMixin:\n"
    "    \"\"\"必须位于 GuangYaExperienceMixin 之前的最终可靠性 mixin。\"\"\"\n"
    "\n"
    "    build_id = \"20260824-r5\"\n"
    "    _channel_retry_base_seconds = 30.0\n"
    "    _channel_retry_max_seconds = 900.0\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _ensure_reliability_state(self) -> None:\n"
    "        if not hasattr(self, \"_reliability_lock\"):\n"
    "            self._reliability_lock = threading.RLock()\n"
    "        if not hasattr(self, \"_channel_refresh_lock\"):\n"
    "            self._channel_refresh_lock = threading.Lock()\n"
    "        if not hasattr(self, \"_async_route_active\"):\n"
    "            self._async_route_active: set[int] = set()\n"
    "        if not hasattr(self, \"_async_route_recheck\"):\n"
    "            self._async_route_recheck: set[int] = set()\n"
    "        if not hasattr(self, \"_channel_recovery_timer\"):\n"
    "            self._channel_recovery_timer = None\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _claim_runtime_owner(self) -> None:\n"
    "        \"\"\"把所有热重载实例的唯一所有权锚定到 MoviePilot 稳定类对象上。\"\"\"\n"
    "        self._runtime_generation = f\"{time.time_ns()}-{id(self)}\"\n"
    "        SubscribeChain._guangya_runtime_owner_ref = weakref.ref(self)\n"
    "        SubscribeChain._guangya_runtime_generation = self._runtime_generation\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _runtime_is_current(self) -> bool:\n"
    "        ref = getattr(SubscribeChain, \"_guangya_runtime_owner_ref\", None)\n"
    "        owner = ref() if callable(ref) else None\n"
    "        return owner is self and str(getattr(SubscribeChain, \"_guangya_runtime_generation\", \"\")) == str(\n"
    "            getattr(self, \"_runtime_generation\", \"\")\n"
    "        )\n"
    "\n"
    "    def init_plugin(self, config: dict = None) -> None:\n"
    "        self._ensure_reliability_state()\n"
    "        previous_ref = getattr(SubscribeChain, \"_guangya_runtime_owner_ref\", None)\n"
    "        previous_generation = getattr(SubscribeChain, \"_guangya_runtime_generation\", \"\")\n"
    "        self._claim_runtime_owner()\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            super().init_plugin(config)\n"
    "        except Exception:\n"
    "            # 初始化失败不能让坏实例抢占运行所有权。\n"
    "            SubscribeChain._guangya_runtime_owner_ref = previous_ref\n"
    "            SubscribeChain._guangya_runtime_generation = previous_generation\n"
    "            raise\n"
    "        self._record_route_health(\n"
    "            runtime_owner=True,\n"
    "            runtime_generation=self._runtime_generation,\n"
    "            runtime_started_at=self._now_text(),\n"
    "            build=self.build_id,\n"
    "        )\n"
    "\n"
    "    @eventmanager.register(EventType.PluginAction)\n"
    "    def action_event_handler(self, event: Event) -> None:\n"
    "        \"\"\"热重载后旧实例即使残留回调，也不再处理旧版消息 action。\"\"\"\n"
    "        if not self._runtime_is_current():\n"
    "            return\n"
    "        return super().action_event_handler(event)\n"
    "\n"
    "    @eventmanager.register(EventType.PluginAction)\n"
    "    def experience_action_event_handler(self, event: Event) -> None:\n"
    "        \"\"\"热重载后旧实例即使残留回调，也不再处理体验层消息 action。\"\"\"\n"
    "        if not self._runtime_is_current():\n"
    "            return\n"
    "        return super().experience_action_event_handler(event)\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _cached_channel_items(self) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:\n"
    "        index = self.get_data(\"channel_index\") or {}\n"
    "        return list(index.get(\"items\") or [])\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _channel_outage_state(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        return dict(self.get_data(\"channel_outage\") or {})\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _schedule_channel_recovery(self, delay: float) -> None:\n"
    "        \"\"\"频道故障后即使没有新的 MP 搜索事件，也会在退避到期后自动尝试恢复。\"\"\"\n"
    "        self._ensure_reliability_state()\n"
    "        delay = max(1.0, float(delay or 1.0))\n"
    "        with self._reliability_lock:\n"
    "            current = self._channel_recovery_timer\n"
    "            if current and current.is_alive():\n"
    "                return\n"
    "\n"
    "            def recover() -> None:\n"
    "                try:\n"
    "                    if not self._runtime_is_current() or not self._enabled:\n"
    "                        return\n"
    "                    ids = list(sorted(set(int(v) for v in self._selected_subscriptions if int(v or 0) > 0)))\n"
    "                    if ids:\n"
    "                        self._plugin_log(\n"
    "                            \"INFO\",\n"
    "                            \"【光鸭转存助手】【频道恢复】退避时间已到，自动重新检查 %s 个固定转存订阅\",\n"
    "                            len(ids),\n"
    "                        )\n"
    "                        self._queue_async_route_check(ids, trigger=\"频道故障自动恢复\")\n"
    "                    else:\n"
    "                        # 没有固定订阅时也刷新一次索引，恢复频道健康状态。\n"
    "                        self.refresh_channels(force=True)\n"
    "                finally:\n"
    "                    with self._reliability_lock:\n"
    "                        self._channel_recovery_timer = None\n"
    "\n"
    "            timer = threading.Timer(delay, recover)\n"
    "            timer.daemon = True\n"
    "            self._channel_recovery_timer = timer\n"
    "            timer.start()\n"
    "\n"
    "    def refresh_channels(self, force: bool = False):\n"
    "        \"\"\"频道刷新单飞 + 故障熔断。\n"
    "\n"
    "        - 同一进程只允许一个频道刷新在跑，其它调用直接复用缓存；\n"
    "        - 全源失败时保留旧索引，不把固定转存误回退到本地下载；\n"
    "        - 指数退避后自动恢复，避免镜像故障时被高频 search 打爆。\n"
    "        \"\"\"\n"
    "        self._ensure_reliability_state()\n"
    "        now = time.time()\n"
    "        outage = self._channel_outage_state()\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            retry_after = float(outage.get(\"retry_after\") or 0)\n"
    "        except (TypeError, ValueError):\n"
    "            retry_after = 0.0\n"
    "        if retry_after > now:\n"
    "            wait = max(1, int(retry_after - now))\n"
    "            self._record_route_health(\n"
    "                channel_degraded=True,\n"
    "                channel_retry_after=retry_after,\n"
    "                channel_retry_seconds=wait,\n"
    "            )\n"
    "            return self._cached_channel_items()\n"
    "\n"
    "        if not self._channel_refresh_lock.acquire(blocking=False):\n"
    "            self._record_route_health(channel_refresh_coalesced_at=self._now_text())\n"
    "            return self._cached_channel_items()\n"
    "\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            try:\n"
    "                entries = super().refresh_channels(force=force)\n"
    "            except Exception as err:\n"
    "                last_run = {\"success\": False, \"errors\": [str(err)]}\n"
    "                entries = self._cached_channel_items()\n"
    "            else:\n"
    "                last_run = self.get_data(\"last_run\") or {}\n"
    "\n"
    "            if bool(last_run.get(\"success\")):\n"
    "                previous_failures = int(outage.get(\"failures\") or 0)\n"
    "                self.save_data(\"channel_outage\", {\n"
    "                    \"state\": \"healthy\",\n"
    "                    \"failures\": 0,\n"
    "                    \"recovered_at\": self._now_text(),\n"
    "                    \"retry_after\": 0,\n"
    "                })\n"
    "                self._record_route_health(\n"
    "                    channel_degraded=False,\n"
    "                    channel_failures=0,\n"
    "                    channel_recovered_at=self._now_text(),\n"
    "                )\n"
    "                if previous_failures:\n"
    "                    self._plugin_log(\n"
    "                        \"INFO\",\n"
    "                        \"【光鸭转存助手】【频道恢复】频道源已恢复，结束缓存降级模式（此前连续失败 %s 次）\",\n"
    "                        previous_failures,\n"
    "                    )\n"
    "                return entries\n"
    "\n"
    "            failures = max(1, int(outage.get(\"failures\") or 0) + 1)\n"
    "            delay = min(\n"
    "                self._channel_retry_max_seconds,\n"
    "                self._channel_retry_base_seconds * (2 ** min(failures - 1, 5)),\n"
    "            )\n"
    "            retry_at = time.time() + delay\n"
    "            errors = last_run.get(\"errors\") or []\n"
    "            self.save_data(\"channel_outage\", {\n"
    "                \"state\": \"degraded\",\n"
    "                \"failures\": failures,\n"
    "                \"since\": outage.get(\"since\") or self._now_text(),\n"
    "                \"last_failure\": self._now_text(),\n"
    "                \"retry_after\": retry_at,\n"
    "                \"retry_seconds\": int(delay),\n"
    "                \"errors\": list(errors)[-5:],\n"
    "                \"cached_items\": len(self._cached_channel_items()),\n"
    "            })\n"
    "            self._record_route_health(\n"
    "                channel_degraded=True,\n"
    "                channel_failures=failures,\n"
    "                channel_retry_after=retry_at,\n"
    "            )\n"
    "            self._plugin_log(\n"
    "                \"WARNING\",\n"
    "                \"【光鸭转存助手】【频道降级】频道源连续失败 %s 次，继续使用本地缓存；%s 秒后自动重试，不回退本地下载\",\n"
    "                failures,\n"
    "                int(delay),\n"
    "            )\n"
    "            self._schedule_channel_recovery(delay)\n"
    "            return entries or self._cached_channel_items()\n"
    "        finally:\n"
    "            self._channel_refresh_lock.release()\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _run_reliability_route_batch(self, batch: List[int], trigger: str) -> None:\n"
    "        subscriptions = []\n"
    "        need_refresh = False\n"
    "        for sid in batch:\n"
    "            if not self._runtime_is_current() or not self._enabled:\n"
    "                return\n"
    "            subscribe = self._find_subscription(sid)\n"
    "            if not subscribe or not self._is_guangya_route(subscribe):\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            subscriptions.append(subscribe)\n"
    "            try:\n"
    "                if not self._cached_matches_for_subscription(subscribe):\n"
    "                    need_refresh = True\n"
    "            except Exception:\n"
    "                need_refresh = True\n"
    "\n"
    "        if need_refresh and subscriptions:\n"
    "            self._plugin_log(\n"
    "                \"INFO\",\n"
    "                \"【光鸭转存助手】【后台合并】%s：%s 个订阅统一执行一次频道检查\",\n"
    "                trigger,\n"
    "                len(subscriptions),\n"
    "            )\n"
    "            self.refresh_channels(force=True)\n"
    "\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            self._inspect_cache.clear()\n"
    "        except Exception:\n"
    "            pass\n"
    "\n"
    "        for subscribe in subscriptions:\n"
    "            if not self._runtime_is_current() or not self._enabled:\n"
    "                return\n"
    "            sid = int(getattr(subscribe, \"id\", 0) or 0)\n"
    "            try:\n"
    "                result = self._try_transfer_subscription(subscribe, refresh_channel=False)\n"
    "                message = str(result.get(\"message\") or \"检查完成\")\n"
    "                self._record_route_health(\n"
    "                    last_route_result=message[:500],\n"
    "                    last_route_result_at=self._now_text(),\n"
    "                    last_async_check_id=sid,\n"
    "                    last_async_trigger=trigger,\n"
    "                )\n"
    "                self._plugin_log(\n"
    "                    \"INFO\",\n"
    "                    \"【光鸭转存助手】【后台检查】#%s %s：%s\",\n"
    "                    sid,\n"
    "                    getattr(subscribe, \"name\", \"\"),\n"
    "                    message,\n"
    "                )\n"
    "            except Exception as err:\n"
    "                self._plugin_log(\n"
    "                    \"EXCEPTION\",\n"
    "                    \"【光鸭转存助手】【后台检查】#%s %s 异常：%s\",\n"
    "                    sid,\n"
    "                    getattr(subscribe, \"name\", \"\"),\n"
    "                    err,\n"
    "                )\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _queue_async_route_check(self, sids: Iterable[int], trigger: str = \"后台检查\") -> None:\n"
    "        \"\"\"可靠的后台检查合并器：同一订阅执行期间最多只保留一次补偿重查。\"\"\"\n"
    "        self._ensure_reliability_state()\n"
    "        ids = {\n"
    "            int(value) for value in sids\n"
    "            if str(value).isdigit() and int(value) > 0\n"
    "        }\n"
    "        if not ids or not self._enabled or not self._runtime_is_current():\n"
    "            return\n"
    "\n"
    "        with self._async_route_lock:\n"
    "            active_hits = ids.intersection(self._async_route_active)\n"
    "            if active_hits:\n"
    "                self._async_route_recheck.update(active_hits)\n"
    "            self._async_route_pending.update(ids - active_hits)\n"
    "            if self._async_route_worker_running:\n"
    "                return\n"
    "            if not self._async_route_pending:\n"
    "                return\n"
    "            self._async_route_worker_running = True\n"
    "\n"
    "        def worker() -> None:\n"
    "            try:\n"
    "                time.sleep(float(getattr(self, \"_async_route_debounce\", 0.25) or 0.25))\n"
    "                while self._enabled and self._runtime_is_current():\n"
    "                    with self._async_route_lock:\n"
    "                        batch = sorted(self._async_route_pending)\n"
    "                        self._async_route_pending.clear()\n"
    "                        self._async_route_active = set(batch)\n"
    "                    if not batch:\n"
    "                        break\n"
    "\n"
    "                    self._run_reliability_route_batch(batch, trigger)\n"
    "\n"
    "                    with self._async_route_lock:\n"
    "                        self._async_route_active.clear()\n"
    "                        if self._async_route_recheck:\n"
    "                            self._async_route_pending.update(self._async_route_recheck)\n"
    "                            self._async_route_recheck.clear()\n"
    "                        has_more = bool(self._async_route_pending)\n"
    "                    if not has_more:\n"
    "                        break\n"
    "                    time.sleep(0.05)\n"
    "            finally:\n"
    "                relaunch: List[int] = []\n"
    "                with self._async_route_lock:\n"
    "                    self._async_route_active.clear()\n"
    "                    self._async_route_worker_running = False\n"
    "                    if self._async_route_recheck:\n"
    "                        self._async_route_pending.update(self._async_route_recheck)\n"
    "                        self._async_route_recheck.clear()\n"
    "                    if self._async_route_pending and self._enabled and self._runtime_is_current():\n"
    "                        relaunch = sorted(self._async_route_pending)\n"
    "                # 关键修复：补偿启动必须带真实 ID，不能用空列表，否则队列会永久卡住。\n"
    "                if relaunch:\n"
    "                    self._queue_async_route_check(relaunch, trigger=\"后台合并补偿\")\n"
    "\n"
    "        threading.Thread(target=worker, name=\"GuangYaReliableRouteCheck\", daemon=True).start()\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _diagnose_subscription(self, subscribe: Any) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        row = dict(super()._diagnose_subscription(subscribe))\n"
    "        outage = self._channel_outage_state()\n"
    "        if str(outage.get(\"state\") or \"\") == \"degraded\" and not row.get(\"pending_jobs\"):\n"
    "            try:\n"
    "                retry_after = float(outage.get(\"retry_after\") or 0)\n"
    "                wait = max(0, int(retry_after - time.time()))\n"
    "            except (TypeError, ValueError):\n"
    "                wait = 0\n"
    "            if not row.get(\"matches\"):\n"
    "                row[\"reason\"] = (\n"
    "                    f\"频道源暂时不可用，正在使用本地缓存降级；约 {wait} 秒后自动恢复检查。\"\n"
    "                    \"固定分流仍生效，不会因此转为本地下载\"\n"
    "                )\n"
    "                row[\"severity\"] = \"warning\"\n"
    "        return row\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _build_selfcheck(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        report = dict(super()._build_selfcheck())\n"
    "        checks = list(report.get(\"checks\") or [])\n"
    "        owner_ok = self._runtime_is_current()\n"
    "        checks.append({\n"
    "            \"key\": \"runtime_owner\",\n"
    "            \"label\": \"热重载实例所有权\",\n"
    "            \"ok\": owner_ok,\n"
    "            \"detail\": \"当前实例为唯一运行所有者\" if owner_ok else \"当前实例已被新版本替代\",\n"
    "            \"critical\": True,\n"
    "        })\n"
    "        outage = self._channel_outage_state()\n"
    "        degraded = str(outage.get(\"state\") or \"\") == \"degraded\"\n"
    "        if degraded:\n"
    "            try:\n"
    "                wait = max(0, int(float(outage.get(\"retry_after\") or 0) - time.time()))\n"
    "            except (TypeError, ValueError):\n"
    "                wait = 0\n"
    "            detail = (\n"
    "                f\"缓存降级中，连续失败 {int(outage.get('failures') or 0)} 次，\"\n"
    "                f\"缓存 {int(outage.get('cached_items') or 0)} 条，约 {wait} 秒后自动重试\"\n"
    "            )\n"
    "        else:\n"
    "            detail = \"频道刷新健康，故障熔断未触发\"\n"
    "        checks.append({\n"
    "            \"key\": \"channel_circuit\",\n"
    "            \"label\": \"频道故障降级/恢复\",\n"
    "            \"ok\": not degraded,\n"
    "            \"detail\": detail,\n"
    "            \"critical\": False,\n"
    "        })\n"
    "        report[\"checks\"] = checks\n"
    "        report[\"healthy\"] = not any(item.get(\"critical\") and not item.get(\"ok\") for item in checks)\n"
    "        report[\"build\"] = self.build_id\n"
    "        report[\"channel_degraded\"] = degraded\n"
    "        return report\n"
    "\n"
    "    def get_page(self):\n"
    "        pages = list(super().get_page() or [])\n"
    "        outage = self._channel_outage_state()\n"
    "        if str(outage.get(\"state\") or \"\") != \"degraded\":\n"
    "            return pages\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            wait = max(0, int(float(outage.get(\"retry_after\") or 0) - time.time()))\n"
    "        except (TypeError, ValueError):\n"
    "            wait = 0\n"
    "        alert = {\n"
    "            \"component\": \"VAlert\",\n"
    "            \"props\": {\n"
    "                \"type\": \"warning\",\n"
    "                \"variant\": \"tonal\",\n"
    "                \"class\": \"mb-3\",\n"
    "                \"title\": \"频道源暂时不可用 · 已进入缓存降级\",\n"
    "                \"text\": (\n"
    "                    f\"连续失败 {int(outage.get('failures') or 0)} 次，仍保留本地频道索引，\"\n"
    "                    f\"约 {wait} 秒后自动重试。光鸭固定分流和本地下载断路器继续生效，不会静默回退。\"\n"
    "                ),\n"
    "            },\n"
    "        }\n"
    "        return [alert, *pages]\n"
    "\n"
    "    def stop_service(self) -> None:\n"
    "        self._ensure_reliability_state()\n"
    "        with self._reliability_lock:\n"
    "            timer = self._channel_recovery_timer\n"
    "            self._channel_recovery_timer = None\n"
    "        if timer and timer.is_alive():\n"
    "            try:\n"
    "                timer.cancel()\n"
    "            except Exception:\n"
    "                pass\n"
    "        current = self._runtime_is_current()\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            return super().stop_service()\n"
    "        finally:\n"
    "            if current and self._runtime_is_current():\n"
    "                SubscribeChain._guangya_runtime_owner_ref = None\n"
    "                SubscribeChain._guangya_runtime_generation = \"\"\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "__all__ = [\"GuangYaReliabilityMixin\"]\n"
    "\n"
)

# ===== runtime_v170.py =====
_INLINE_MODULE_SOURCES["runtime_v170"] = (
    "\"\"\"光鸭转存助手 v1.7.0 最终运行编排层。\n"
    "\n"
    "把 legacy/routing/experience/reliability 已有能力统一收口到一个运行语义：\n"
    "- 宿主 scheduler takeover 与 SubscribeChain.search 一样只做硬分流，绝不在搜索线程同步跑网盘转存；\n"
    "- 所有周期批量检查统一进入可靠后台合并队列；\n"
    "- 旧热重载实例的 tick/刷新/新转存入口与内置守护线程全部失效；\n"
    "- scheduler takeover 跨热重载会追溯真实 MoviePilot 原回调，不形成“旧插件回调链”；\n"
    "- 修正频道自动恢复定时器自我占用导致“只重试一次”的边界；\n"
    "- 接管已有订阅前做兼容性预检，避免洗版/复杂规则订阅被切进一个必然不会执行的路线；\n"
    "- /gystatus 展示 search/RSS/最终下载三层门禁、运行实例和频道降级状态；\n"
    "- 完成订阅自动清理固定路线时沿用延迟持久化，避免完成回调中热重载；\n"
    "- 诊断按媒体事实键读取 transfer_jobs，避免任务明明待落盘却显示 0。\n"
    "\"\"\"\n"
    "\n"
    "from __future__ import annotations\n"
    "\n"
    "import inspect\n"
    "import threading\n"
    "import time\n"
    "from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional, Tuple\n"
    "\n"
    "from app.chain.subscribe import SubscribeChain\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "class GuangYaRuntimeFinalizerMixin:\n"
    "    \"\"\"位于 GuangYaReliabilityMixin 之前的最终运行编排。\"\"\"\n"
    "\n"
    "    build_id = \"20260824-r9\"\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _schedule_channel_recovery(self, delay: float) -> None:\n"
    "        \"\"\"可连续重试的恢复定时器。\"\"\"\n"
    "        self._ensure_reliability_state()\n"
    "        delay = max(1.0, float(delay or 1.0))\n"
    "        with self._reliability_lock:\n"
    "            current = self._channel_recovery_timer\n"
    "            if current and current.is_alive():\n"
    "                return\n"
    "\n"
    "            timer = None\n"
    "\n"
    "            def recover() -> None:\n"
    "                with self._reliability_lock:\n"
    "                    if self._channel_recovery_timer is timer:\n"
    "                        self._channel_recovery_timer = None\n"
    "                if not self._runtime_is_current() or not self._enabled:\n"
    "                    return\n"
    "                ids = list(sorted(set(\n"
    "                    int(value) for value in self._selected_subscriptions\n"
    "                    if str(value).isdigit() and int(value) > 0\n"
    "                )))\n"
    "                if ids:\n"
    "                    self._plugin_log(\n"
    "                        \"INFO\",\n"
    "                        \"【光鸭转存助手】【频道恢复】退避到期，自动重新检查 %s 个固定转存订阅\",\n"
    "                        len(ids),\n"
    "                    )\n"
    "                    self._queue_async_route_check(ids, trigger=\"频道故障自动恢复\")\n"
    "                else:\n"
    "                    self.refresh_channels(force=True)\n"
    "\n"
    "            timer = threading.Timer(delay, recover)\n"
    "            timer.daemon = True\n"
    "            self._channel_recovery_timer = timer\n"
    "            timer.start()\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _unwrap_takeover_original(self, job_id: str, func: Any) -> Any:\n"
    "        \"\"\"沿旧插件实例的 _takeover_originals 追溯真正 MoviePilot 原调度函数。\"\"\"\n"
    "        current = func\n"
    "        seen = set()\n"
    "        for _ in range(8):\n"
    "            marker = id(current)\n"
    "            if marker in seen:\n"
    "                break\n"
    "            seen.add(marker)\n"
    "            owner = getattr(current, \"__self__\", None)\n"
    "            if owner is None or owner is self:\n"
    "                break\n"
    "            mapping = getattr(owner, \"_takeover_originals\", None)\n"
    "            if not isinstance(mapping, dict):\n"
    "                break\n"
    "            candidate = mapping.get(job_id)\n"
    "            if candidate is None or candidate is current:\n"
    "                break\n"
    "            current = candidate\n"
    "        return current\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _install_takeover(self) -> None:\n"
    "        \"\"\"安装 scheduler takeover，并修正热重载产生的旧实例回调链。\"\"\"\n"
    "        if not self._runtime_is_current() or not self._enabled:\n"
    "            return\n"
    "        super()._install_takeover()\n"
    "        originals = dict(getattr(self, \"_takeover_originals\", {}) or {})\n"
    "        for job_id, original in originals.items():\n"
    "            unwrapped = self._unwrap_takeover_original(job_id, original)\n"
    "            if unwrapped is original:\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            self._takeover_originals[job_id] = unwrapped\n"
    "            self._plugin_log(\n"
    "                \"INFO\",\n"
    "                \"【光鸭转存助手】【热重载】%s 已从旧插件回调链追溯到 MoviePilot 原调度函数\",\n"
    "                job_id,\n"
    "            )\n"
    "\n"
    "    def refresh_channels(self, force: bool = False):\n"
    "        \"\"\"旧热重载实例不再触碰频道索引或外部网络。\"\"\"\n"
    "        if hasattr(self, \"_runtime_generation\") and not self._runtime_is_current():\n"
    "            return self._cached_channel_items()\n"
    "        return super().refresh_channels(force=force)\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _runtime_worker_loop(self, generation: int) -> None:\n"
    "        \"\"\"内置守护使用稳定 runtime owner，而不是仅依赖热重载后会重建的 Python class generation。\"\"\"\n"
    "        stop = getattr(self, \"_runtime_stop\", None)\n"
    "        if stop is None:\n"
    "            return\n"
    "        if stop.wait(1.5):\n"
    "            return\n"
    "        if not self._runtime_is_current() or not self._enabled:\n"
    "            return\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            self._plugin_log(\"INFO\", \"【光鸭转存助手】【启动检查】内置守护开始首轮缓存检查\")\n"
    "            self._startup_check()\n"
    "        except Exception as err:\n"
    "            self._plugin_log(\"EXCEPTION\", \"【光鸭转存助手】【启动检查】内置守护首轮执行异常：%s\", err)\n"
    "\n"
    "        while self._enabled and self._runtime_is_current():\n"
    "            interval = max(60, int(self._refresh_minutes or 5) * 60)\n"
    "            if stop.wait(interval):\n"
    "                return\n"
    "            if not self._runtime_is_current() or not self._enabled:\n"
    "                return\n"
    "            heartbeat = float(getattr(self, \"_host_tick_heartbeat\", 0.0) or 0.0)\n"
    "            if heartbeat and (time.monotonic() - heartbeat) < interval * 1.5:\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            try:\n"
    "                self._plugin_log(\n"
    "                    \"WARNING\",\n"
    "                    \"【光鸭转存助手】【服务回退】未检测到宿主定时服务心跳，内置守护执行本轮检查；无需手动保存配置\",\n"
    "                )\n"
    "                self._tick(host_service=False)\n"
    "            except Exception as err:\n"
    "                self._plugin_log(\"EXCEPTION\", \"【光鸭转存助手】【服务回退】内置守护执行异常：%s\", err)\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _startup_check(self) -> None:\n"
    "        if not self._runtime_is_current() or not self._enabled:\n"
    "            return\n"
    "        return super()._startup_check()\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _tick(self, host_service: bool = True) -> None:\n"
    "        \"\"\"宿主/内置定时器只允许最新实例执行。\"\"\"\n"
    "        if not self._runtime_is_current() or not self._enabled:\n"
    "            return\n"
    "        return super()._tick(host_service=host_service)\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _process_selected_subscriptions(\n"
    "        self,\n"
    "        trigger: str = \"后台检查\",\n"
    "        refresh_channel: bool = False,\n"
    "    ) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:\n"
    "        \"\"\"所有批量周期处理统一排入后台，不再和 search/tick 并发直接提交转存。\"\"\"\n"
    "        if not self._runtime_is_current() or not self._enabled:\n"
    "            return []\n"
    "        ids = []\n"
    "        rows = []\n"
    "        for raw_sid in list(self._selected_subscriptions):\n"
    "            try:\n"
    "                sid = int(raw_sid)\n"
    "            except (TypeError, ValueError):\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            subscribe = self._find_subscription(sid)\n"
    "            if not subscribe:\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            state = str(getattr(subscribe, \"state\", \"\") or \"\")\n"
    "            if state not in (\"N\", \"R\"):\n"
    "                rows.append({\n"
    "                    \"subscribe_id\": sid,\n"
    "                    \"success\": True,\n"
    "                    \"handled\": True,\n"
    "                    \"queued\": False,\n"
    "                    \"message\": f\"订阅状态 {state or '-'} 非活跃，固定路线保留但不执行\",\n"
    "                })\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            ids.append(sid)\n"
    "            rows.append({\n"
    "                \"subscribe_id\": sid,\n"
    "                \"success\": True,\n"
    "                \"handled\": True,\n"
    "                \"queued\": True,\n"
    "                \"message\": \"已进入光鸭后台合并队列\",\n"
    "            })\n"
    "        if ids:\n"
    "            self._queue_async_route_check(ids, trigger=trigger)\n"
    "        return rows\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _dispatch_subscribe_search(self, *args, progress_callback=None, **kwargs):\n"
    "        \"\"\"宿主 scheduler takeover 的最终固定分流。\n"
    "\n"
    "        - 显式 progress_callback：供 Scheduler inspect.signature 检测；\n"
    "        - 位置参数只归一化一次，之后一律 **kwargs 转发，禁止 *args + 同名 kwargs；\n"
    "        - 周期扫描 due 失败时 fail-closed / 交还原生，禁止全量→sids。\n"
    "        \"\"\"\n"
    "        if not self._runtime_is_current() or not self._enabled:\n"
    "            return True\n"
    "\n"
    "        if progress_callback is not None:\n"
    "            kwargs.setdefault(\"progress_callback\", progress_callback)\n"
    "\n"
    "        probe = SubscribeChain()\n"
    "        normalizer = getattr(self, \"_normalize_search_call\", None)\n"
    "        if callable(normalizer):\n"
    "            forward_kwargs = dict(\n"
    "                normalizer(SubscribeChain.search, probe, args, kwargs) or {}\n"
    "            )\n"
    "        else:\n"
    "            # 极端兜底：无路由层时仍避免 *args 与 kwargs 重复\n"
    "            forward_kwargs = dict(kwargs)\n"
    "            if args:\n"
    "                try:\n"
    "                    bound = inspect.signature(SubscribeChain.search).bind_partial(\n"
    "                        probe, *args, **forward_kwargs\n"
    "                    )\n"
    "                    bound.apply_defaults()\n"
    "                    forward_kwargs = dict(bound.arguments)\n"
    "                    forward_kwargs.pop(\"self\", None)\n"
    "                except TypeError:\n"
    "                    self._plugin_log(\n"
    "                        \"ERROR\",\n"
    "                        \"【光鸭转存助手】【调度分流】位置参数归一化失败；本轮取消\",\n"
    "                    )\n"
    "                    return True\n"
    "\n"
    "        sid = forward_kwargs.get(\"sid\")\n"
    "        state = forward_kwargs.get(\"state\", \"R\")\n"
    "        manual = forward_kwargs.get(\"manual\", False)\n"
    "        progress_callback = forward_kwargs.get(\"progress_callback\")\n"
    "        sids = forward_kwargs.get(\"sids\")\n"
    "        scheduled_interval = forward_kwargs.get(\"scheduled_interval\")\n"
    "\n"
    "        selected = set(int(value) for value in self._selected_subscriptions if str(value).isdigit())\n"
    "        is_managed = getattr(self, \"_is_managed_subscription\", None)\n"
    "        is_managed_sid = getattr(self, \"_is_managed_sid\", None)\n"
    "\n"
    "        def _managed(sub_or_id) -> bool:\n"
    "            if callable(is_managed):\n"
    "                return bool(is_managed(sub_or_id))\n"
    "            if callable(is_managed_sid) and (\n"
    "                isinstance(sub_or_id, (int, str)) and str(sub_or_id).isdigit()\n"
    "            ):\n"
    "                return bool(is_managed_sid(sub_or_id))\n"
    "            try:\n"
    "                return int(getattr(sub_or_id, \"id\", sub_or_id) or 0) in selected\n"
    "            except (TypeError, ValueError):\n"
    "                return False\n"
    "\n"
    "        if sid is not None:\n"
    "            current_sid = int(sid)\n"
    "            if _managed(current_sid):\n"
    "                subscribe = self._find_subscription(current_sid)\n"
    "                active = bool(\n"
    "                    subscribe and str(getattr(subscribe, \"state\", \"\") or \"\") in (\"N\", \"R\")\n"
    "                )\n"
    "                if active:\n"
    "                    self._queue_async_route_check([current_sid], trigger=\"宿主订阅搜索分流\")\n"
    "                    self._plugin_log(\n"
    "                        \"INFO\",\n"
    "                        \"【光鸭转存助手】【调度分流】#%s %s 已阻断原生搜索并转入后台光鸭检查\",\n"
    "                        current_sid,\n"
    "                        getattr(subscribe, \"name\", \"\") if subscribe else \"\",\n"
    "                    )\n"
    "                    if progress_callback:\n"
    "                        progress_callback(value=100, text=\"固定转存订阅已交由光鸭后台检查\")\n"
    "                else:\n"
    "                    self._plugin_log(\n"
    "                        \"INFO\",\n"
    "                        \"【光鸭转存助手】【原生阻断】【调度分流】固定转存 #%s state=%s 非活跃，仍跳过原生搜索\",\n"
    "                        current_sid,\n"
    "                        str(getattr(subscribe, \"state\", \"\") or \"-\") if subscribe else \"-\",\n"
    "                    )\n"
    "                return True\n"
    "            return SubscribeChain().search(**forward_kwargs)\n"
    "\n"
    "        is_targeted = sids is not None\n"
    "        is_scheduled_scan = (\n"
    "            (not is_targeted)\n"
    "            and scheduled_interval is not None\n"
    "            and not bool(manual)\n"
    "        )\n"
    "\n"
    "        route_ids: List[int] = []\n"
    "        native_ids: List[int] = []\n"
    "        if sids is not None:\n"
    "            subscriptions = [self._find_subscription(int(value)) for value in sids]\n"
    "            subscriptions = [item for item in subscriptions if item is not None]\n"
    "            for raw in sids:\n"
    "                try:\n"
    "                    mid = int(raw)\n"
    "                except (TypeError, ValueError):\n"
    "                    continue\n"
    "                if mid > 0 and _managed(mid) and all(\n"
    "                    int(getattr(item, \"id\", 0) or 0) != mid for item in subscriptions\n"
    "                ):\n"
    "                    route_ids.append(mid)\n"
    "        elif is_scheduled_scan:\n"
    "            loader = getattr(self, \"_load_due_search_subscriptions\", None)\n"
    "            if not callable(loader):\n"
    "                self._plugin_log(\n"
    "                    \"ERROR\",\n"
    "                    \"【光鸭转存助手】【调度分流】【due失败】缺少 due 加载器；本轮取消周期搜索，禁止全量兜底\",\n"
    "                )\n"
    "                if progress_callback:\n"
    "                    progress_callback(value=100, text=\"宿主到期筛选不可用，本轮周期搜索已取消\")\n"
    "                return True\n"
    "            subscriptions = loader(\n"
    "                probe, state=state or \"R\", scheduled_interval=scheduled_interval\n"
    "            )\n"
    "            if subscriptions is None:\n"
    "                self._plugin_log(\n"
    "                    \"ERROR\",\n"
    "                    \"【光鸭转存助手】【调度分流】【due失败】本轮取消周期搜索；禁止全量列举或 targeted sids 兜底\",\n"
    "                )\n"
    "                if progress_callback:\n"
    "                    progress_callback(value=100, text=\"宿主到期筛选失败，本轮周期搜索已取消\")\n"
    "                return True\n"
    "            subscriptions = list(subscriptions or [])\n"
    "            self._plugin_log(\n"
    "                \"INFO\",\n"
    "                \"【光鸭转存助手】【调度分流】周期扫描宿主 due 后候选=%s interval=%s\",\n"
    "                len(subscriptions),\n"
    "                scheduled_interval,\n"
    "            )\n"
    "        else:\n"
    "            subscriptions = list(self._list_subscriptions(state or \"N,R\") or [])\n"
    "\n"
    "        for subscribe in subscriptions:\n"
    "            current_sid = int(getattr(subscribe, \"id\", 0) or 0)\n"
    "            if not current_sid:\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            if _managed(subscribe):\n"
    "                route_ids.append(current_sid)\n"
    "            else:\n"
    "                native_ids.append(current_sid)\n"
    "\n"
    "        if route_ids:\n"
    "            active_route = []\n"
    "            for rid in route_ids:\n"
    "                sub = self._find_subscription(rid)\n"
    "                if sub and str(getattr(sub, \"state\", \"\") or \"\") in (\"N\", \"R\"):\n"
    "                    active_route.append(rid)\n"
    "            if active_route:\n"
    "                self._queue_async_route_check(active_route, trigger=\"宿主批量订阅搜索分流\")\n"
    "            self._plugin_log(\n"
    "                \"INFO\",\n"
    "                \"【光鸭转存助手】【调度分流】批量阻断 %s 个固定转存订阅的原生搜索；后台检查活跃=%s\",\n"
    "                len(route_ids),\n"
    "                len(active_route),\n"
    "            )\n"
    "\n"
    "        if native_ids:\n"
    "            native_kwargs = dict(forward_kwargs)\n"
    "            native_kwargs.pop(\"sid\", None)\n"
    "            if is_scheduled_scan:\n"
    "                native_kwargs.pop(\"scheduled_interval\", None)\n"
    "            native_kwargs[\"sids\"] = tuple(native_ids)\n"
    "            return SubscribeChain().search(**native_kwargs)\n"
    "        if progress_callback:\n"
    "            progress_callback(value=100, text=\"固定转存订阅已全部交由光鸭后台检查\")\n"
    "        return True\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _runtime_call_native_search(self, **forward_kwargs):\n"
    "        \"\"\"仅在确认 kwargs 不含已接管 sid 时调用原生 search。\"\"\"\n"
    "        managed_sid = getattr(self, \"_is_managed_sid\", None)\n"
    "        selected = set(int(value) for value in self._selected_subscriptions if str(value).isdigit())\n"
    "\n"
    "        def _is_managed(value: Any) -> bool:\n"
    "            if callable(managed_sid):\n"
    "                return bool(managed_sid(value))\n"
    "            try:\n"
    "                return int(value) in selected\n"
    "            except (TypeError, ValueError):\n"
    "                return False\n"
    "\n"
    "        kwargs = dict(forward_kwargs or {})\n"
    "        sid = kwargs.get(\"sid\")\n"
    "        sids = kwargs.get(\"sids\")\n"
    "        if sid is not None and _is_managed(sid):\n"
    "            self._plugin_log(\n"
    "                \"WARNING\",\n"
    "                \"【光鸭转存助手】【原生阻断】拒绝交还已接管 sid=%s 给原生 search\",\n"
    "                sid,\n"
    "            )\n"
    "            return True\n"
    "        if sids is not None:\n"
    "            kept = []\n"
    "            for value in sids:\n"
    "                if _is_managed(value):\n"
    "                    continue\n"
    "                kept.append(int(value))\n"
    "            if not kept:\n"
    "                return True\n"
    "            kwargs[\"sids\"] = tuple(kept)\n"
    "            kwargs[\"sid\"] = None\n"
    "        # 禁止带着 scheduled_interval 把 managed 重新加载回来\n"
    "        kwargs.pop(\"scheduled_interval\", None)\n"
    "\n"
    "        current = SubscribeChain.search\n"
    "        original = getattr(current, \"_guangya_original_search\", None)\n"
    "        chain = SubscribeChain()\n"
    "        if callable(original) and original is not current:\n"
    "            return original(chain, **kwargs)\n"
    "        self._plugin_log(\n"
    "            \"ERROR\",\n"
    "            \"【光鸭转存助手】【调度分流】无法安全交还原生；本轮取消\",\n"
    "        )\n"
    "        return True\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _try_transfer_subscription(\n"
    "        self,\n"
    "        subscribe: Any,\n"
    "        force: bool = False,\n"
    "        refresh_channel: bool = True,\n"
    "    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        \"\"\"旧热重载实例不得再启动新的分享检查/转存提交。\"\"\"\n"
    "        if not self._runtime_is_current():\n"
    "            return {\n"
    "                \"success\": True,\n"
    "                \"handled\": True,\n"
    "                \"stale_instance\": True,\n"
    "                \"message\": \"旧插件实例已被新版本接管，本次不再执行转存\",\n"
    "            }\n"
    "        return super()._try_transfer_subscription(\n"
    "            subscribe,\n"
    "            force=force,\n"
    "            refresh_channel=refresh_channel,\n"
    "        )\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _remove_selected_subscription(self, sid: int) -> None:\n"
    "        \"\"\"所有路线移除（人工切回/订阅完成自动清理）都走安全的延迟配置持久化。\"\"\"\n"
    "        return super()._remove_selected_subscription(sid)\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _route_preflight(self, subscribe: Any) -> Tuple[bool, str]:\n"
    "        \"\"\"接管已有订阅前检查是否存在固定转存无法表达的订阅语义。\"\"\"\n"
    "        allowed, reason = self._subscription_static_guard(subscribe)\n"
    "        if allowed:\n"
    "            return True, \"\"\n"
    "        if str(reason or \"\").startswith(\"订阅状态 \"):\n"
    "            return True, f\"{reason}；路线可保存，恢复订阅后再执行光鸭检查\"\n"
    "        return False, str(reason or \"当前订阅不适合固定转存\")\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _handle_takeover_existing_command(self, event_data: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n"
    "        subscribe = self._command_subscription_or_reply(event_data, selected_only=False)\n"
    "        if not subscribe:\n"
    "            return\n"
    "        sid = int(getattr(subscribe, \"id\", 0) or 0)\n"
    "        if self._is_guangya_route(subscribe):\n"
    "            return super()._handle_takeover_existing_command(event_data)\n"
    "        allowed, reason = self._route_preflight(subscribe)\n"
    "        if not allowed:\n"
    "            self._post_command(\n"
    "                event_data,\n"
    "                \"⛔ 无法切到光鸭固定转存\",\n"
    "                f\"#{sid} {getattr(subscribe, 'name', '')}\\n原因：{reason}\\n已保持 MoviePilot 普通下载路线。\",\n"
    "            )\n"
    "            self._plugin_log(\n"
    "                \"WARNING\",\n"
    "                \"【光鸭转存助手】【路线预检】#%s %s 拒绝接管：%s\",\n"
    "                sid,\n"
    "                getattr(subscribe, \"name\", \"\"),\n"
    "                reason,\n"
    "            )\n"
    "            return\n"
    "        return super()._handle_takeover_existing_command(event_data)\n"
    "\n"
    "    def api_route_guangya(self, subscribe_id: int = 0) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        sid = int(subscribe_id or 0)\n"
    "        subscribe = self._find_subscription(sid)\n"
    "        if not sid or not subscribe:\n"
    "            return {\"success\": False, \"message\": \"订阅不存在\"}\n"
    "        if not self._is_guangya_route(subscribe):\n"
    "            allowed, reason = self._route_preflight(subscribe)\n"
    "            if not allowed:\n"
    "                return {\n"
    "                    \"success\": False,\n"
    "                    \"message\": f\"不能切到光鸭固定转存：{reason}；已保持 MoviePilot 普通下载路线\",\n"
    "                }\n"
    "        return super().api_route_guangya(subscribe_id=sid)\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _handle_status_command(self, event_data: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n"
    "        \"\"\"状态命令显示真正的三层门禁、运行实例、频道状态和任务总览。\"\"\"\n"
    "        report = self._build_selfcheck()\n"
    "        checks = {str(item.get(\"key\") or \"\"): item for item in (report.get(\"checks\") or [])}\n"
    "        index = self.get_data(\"channel_index\") or {}\n"
    "        health = self.get_data(\"route_health\") or {}\n"
    "        outage = self._channel_outage_state()\n"
    "        jobs = [item for item in (self.get_data(\"transfer_jobs\") or {}).values() if isinstance(item, dict)]\n"
    "        pending = sum(1 for item in jobs if str(item.get(\"status\") or \"\") in {\"submitting\", \"submitted\", \"task_confirmed\", \"verifying\"})\n"
    "        failed = sum(1 for item in jobs if str(item.get(\"status\") or \"\") == \"failed\")\n"
    "\n"
    "        def status_icon(key: str) -> str:\n"
    "            item = checks.get(key) or {}\n"
    "            return \"✅\" if item.get(\"ok\") else \"❌\"\n"
    "\n"
    "        channel_text = \"正常\"\n"
    "        if str(outage.get(\"state\") or \"\") == \"degraded\":\n"
    "            try:\n"
    "                wait = max(0, int(float(outage.get(\"retry_after\") or 0) - time.time()))\n"
    "            except (TypeError, ValueError):\n"
    "                wait = 0\n"
    "            channel_text = f\"缓存降级（连续失败 {int(outage.get('failures') or 0)} 次，约 {wait}s 后重试）\"\n"
    "\n"
    "        selected_rows = []\n"
    "        selected_ids = set(self._selected_subscriptions)\n"
    "        for subscribe in self._list_subscriptions(None):\n"
    "            sid = int(getattr(subscribe, \"id\", 0) or 0)\n"
    "            if sid in selected_ids:\n"
    "                selected_rows.append(f\"#{sid} {getattr(subscribe, 'name', '')}\")\n"
    "\n"
    "        text = (\n"
    "            f\"光鸭转存助手 v{getattr(self, 'plugin_version', '1.7.0')} · build {self.build_id}\\n\"\n"
    "            f\"{status_icon('runtime_owner')} 当前运行实例 · {status_icon('search_guard')} Search硬分流 · \"\n"
    "            f\"{status_icon('match_guard')} RSS门禁 · {status_icon('download_guard')} 最终下载断路器\\n\"\n"
    "            f\"频道：{channel_text} · 索引 {len(index.get('items') or [])} 条 · 最近刷新 {index.get('time') or '-'}\\n\"\n"
    "            f\"固定转存：{len(selected_ids)} 个 · 待落盘 {pending} · 失败 {failed}\\n\"\n"
    "            f\"最近处理：{health.get('last_route_result') or '-'}\"\n"
    "        )\n"
    "        if selected_rows:\n"
    "            text += \"\\n\\n当前路线：\\n\" + \"\\n\".join(selected_rows[:12])\n"
    "            if len(selected_rows) > 12:\n"
    "                text += f\"\\n…另有 {len(selected_rows) - 12} 个\"\n"
    "        self._post_command(event_data, \"光鸭转存状态\", text)\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _diagnose_subscription(self, subscribe: Any) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        \"\"\"使用真实媒体事实键关联任务，并优先展示固定转存兼容性阻断原因。\"\"\"\n"
    "        row = dict(super()._diagnose_subscription(subscribe))\n"
    "        prefix = self._media_fact_prefix(subscribe)\n"
    "        jobs = [\n"
    "            dict(item) for item in (self.get_data(\"transfer_jobs\") or {}).values()\n"
    "            if isinstance(item, dict) and str(item.get(\"media\") or \"\") == str(prefix)\n"
    "        ]\n"
    "        pending_status = {\"submitting\", \"submitted\", \"task_confirmed\", \"verifying\"}\n"
    "        pending = [item for item in jobs if str(item.get(\"status\") or \"\") in pending_status]\n"
    "        failed = [item for item in jobs if str(item.get(\"status\") or \"\") == \"failed\"]\n"
    "        row[\"pending_jobs\"] = len(pending)\n"
    "        row[\"failed_jobs\"] = len(failed)\n"
    "        if pending:\n"
    "            row[\"reason\"] = f\"已有 {len(pending)} 个转存任务已提交，正在等待光鸭落盘确认\"\n"
    "            row[\"severity\"] = \"info\"\n"
    "            return row\n"
    "\n"
    "        allowed, reason = self._subscription_static_guard(subscribe)\n"
    "        if not allowed and not str(reason or \"\").startswith(\"订阅状态 \"):\n"
    "            row[\"reason\"] = f\"固定转存规则阻止执行：{reason}\"\n"
    "            row[\"severity\"] = \"warning\"\n"
    "            return row\n"
    "\n"
    "        if failed and not row.get(\"matches\"):\n"
    "            latest = sorted(failed, key=lambda item: str(item.get(\"updated\") or \"\"))[-1]\n"
    "            detail = str(latest.get(\"error\") or latest.get(\"message\") or \"未知错误\")[:180]\n"
    "            row[\"reason\"] = f\"最近转存任务失败：{detail}\"\n"
    "            row[\"severity\"] = \"error\"\n"
    "        return row\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "__all__ = [\"GuangYaRuntimeFinalizerMixin\"]\n"
    "\n"
)

# ===== source_types_v180.py =====
_INLINE_MODULE_SOURCES["source_types_v180"] = (
    "\"\"\"Magnet/ED2K 来源规范化与稳定身份。\"\"\"\n"
    "\n"
    "from __future__ import annotations\n"
    "\n"
    "import base64\n"
    "import hashlib\n"
    "import re\n"
    "from typing import Any, Dict\n"
    "from urllib.parse import parse_qs, unquote, urlsplit\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "SOURCE_TYPES = (\"guangya\", \"magnet\", \"ed2k\")\n"
    "RESOURCE_TYPES = (\"xunlei\", \"guangya\", \"magnet\", \"ed2k\")\n"
    "RESOURCE_EXECUTOR_BY_TYPE = {\n"
    "    \"xunlei\": \"flash_json\",\n"
    "    \"guangya\": \"share_restore\",\n"
    "    \"magnet\": \"cloudcollection\",\n"
    "    \"ed2k\": \"cloudcollection\",\n"
    "}\n"
    "RESOURCE_SOURCE_ORIGINS = (\"telegram\", \"viewing\")\n"
    "SOURCE_SCHEMA_VERSION = 2\n"
    "# queued 已经对应服务端 taskId，必须继续轮询而不是再次 create_task。\n"
    "SOURCE_PENDING_STATES = {\"new\", \"retry\"}\n"
    "SOURCE_INFLIGHT_STATES = {\"dispatching\", \"submitted\", \"queued\", \"waiting\"}\n"
    "# needs_review 表示已解析资源，但集号置信度不足；必须人工确认或换候选，不能自动重试整包。\n"
    "SOURCE_TERMINAL_STATES = {\"completed\", \"failed\", \"disabled\", \"needs_review\"}\n"
    "\n"
    "_BTih_RE = re.compile(r\"^(?:[0-9a-fA-F]{40}|[A-Z2-7a-z2-7]{32})$\")\n"
    "_ED2K_HASH_RE = re.compile(r\"^[0-9a-fA-F]{32}$\")\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "def safe_int(value: Any, default: int = 0, minimum: int = 0) -> int:\n"
    "    try:\n"
    "        result = int(value)\n"
    "    except (TypeError, ValueError):\n"
    "        return default\n"
    "    return max(minimum, result)\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "def resource_executor_for(source_type: str) -> str:\n"
    "    \"\"\"Return the only allowed transfer executor for a discovered resource type.\"\"\"\n"
    "    value = str(source_type or \"\").strip().lower()\n"
    "    executor = RESOURCE_EXECUTOR_BY_TYPE.get(value)\n"
    "    if not executor:\n"
    "        raise ValueError(f\"不支持的资源类型：{source_type}\")\n"
    "    return executor\n"
    "\n"
    "def source_identity(source_type: str, identity: str, subscribe_id: int = 0) -> str:\n"
    "    raw = f\"{int(subscribe_id or 0)}|{source_type}|{identity}\".encode(\"utf-8\")\n"
    "    return hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest()[:24]\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "def normalize_magnet(uri: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "    text = str(uri or \"\").strip()\n"
    "    if not text.lower().startswith(\"magnet:?\"):\n"
    "        raise ValueError(\"不是有效的 Magnet 链接\")\n"
    "    query = parse_qs(urlsplit(text).query, keep_blank_values=True)\n"
    "    btih = \"\"\n"
    "    for value in query.get(\"xt\") or []:\n"
    "        match = re.match(r\"(?i)^urn:btih:([0-9a-z]+)$\", str(value or \"\").strip())\n"
    "        if match and _BTih_RE.fullmatch(match.group(1)):\n"
    "            btih = match.group(1)\n"
    "            break\n"
    "    if not btih:\n"
    "        raise ValueError(\"Magnet 缺少合法的 xt=urn:btih\")\n"
    "    if len(btih) == 32:\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            identity = base64.b32decode(btih.upper()).hex()\n"
    "        except Exception as err:\n"
    "            raise ValueError(\"Magnet BTIH Base32 无法解码\") from err\n"
    "    else:\n"
    "        identity = btih.lower()\n"
    "    name = unquote(str((query.get(\"dn\") or [\"\"])[0] or \"\")).strip()[:300]\n"
    "    size = safe_int((query.get(\"xl\") or [0])[0], 0, 0)\n"
    "    return {\n"
    "        \"type\": \"magnet\",\n"
    "        \"uri\": text,\n"
    "        \"identity\": identity,\n"
    "        \"name\": name,\n"
    "        \"size\": size,\n"
    "    }\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "def normalize_ed2k(uri: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "    text = str(uri or \"\").strip()\n"
    "    if not text.lower().startswith(\"ed2k://|file|\"):\n"
    "        raise ValueError(\"不是有效的 ED2K 文件链接\")\n"
    "    # ED2K 文件链接固定为 ed2k://|file|NAME|SIZE|HASH|/\n"
    "    parts = text.split(\"|\")\n"
    "    if len(parts) < 6 or parts[1].lower() != \"file\":\n"
    "        raise ValueError(\"ED2K 文件链接结构无效\")\n"
    "    name = unquote(parts[2]).strip()\n"
    "    if not name:\n"
    "        raise ValueError(\"ED2K 文件名为空\")\n"
    "    try:\n"
    "        size = int(parts[3])\n"
    "    except (TypeError, ValueError) as err:\n"
    "        raise ValueError(\"ED2K 文件大小无效\") from err\n"
    "    if size <= 0:\n"
    "        raise ValueError(\"ED2K 文件大小无效\")\n"
    "    digest = str(parts[4] or \"\").strip().lower()\n"
    "    if not _ED2K_HASH_RE.fullmatch(digest):\n"
    "        raise ValueError(\"ED2K 文件哈希无效\")\n"
    "    return {\n"
    "        \"type\": \"ed2k\",\n"
    "        \"uri\": text,\n"
    "        \"identity\": digest,\n"
    "        \"name\": name[:300],\n"
    "        \"size\": size,\n"
    "    }\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "def normalize_source_uri(uri: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "    text = str(uri or \"\").strip()\n"
    "    if text.lower().startswith(\"magnet:?\"):\n"
    "        return normalize_magnet(text)\n"
    "    if text.lower().startswith(\"ed2k://|file|\"):\n"
    "        return normalize_ed2k(text)\n"
    "    raise ValueError(\"当前仅支持 magnet:? 与 ed2k://|file| 来源\")\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "__all__ = [\n"
    "    \"SOURCE_TYPES\", \"RESOURCE_TYPES\", \"RESOURCE_EXECUTOR_BY_TYPE\", \"RESOURCE_SOURCE_ORIGINS\",\n"
    "    \"SOURCE_SCHEMA_VERSION\", \"SOURCE_PENDING_STATES\",\n"
    "    \"SOURCE_INFLIGHT_STATES\", \"SOURCE_TERMINAL_STATES\",\n"
    "    \"safe_int\", \"resource_executor_for\", \"source_identity\", \"normalize_magnet\", \"normalize_ed2k\",\n"
    "    \"normalize_source_uri\",\n"
    "]\n"
    "\n"
)
