    "from urllib.parse import urlsplit, urlunsplit, unquote\n"
    "\n"
    "from .source_types_v180 import normalize_source_uri\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "_MAGNET_RE = re.compile(r\"(?i)magnet:\\?[^\\s\\\"'<>]+\")\n"
    "# 允许 HTML 实体解码后的 |，以及 URL 编码残留经 unquote 后的完整 ed2k\n"
    "_ED2K_RE = re.compile(\n"
    "    r\"(?i)ed2k://\\|file\\|[^|\\r\\n<>]+\\|\\d+\\|[0-9a-fA-F]{32}\\|/\"\n"
    ")\n"
    "_ATTR_URL_RE = re.compile(\n"
    "    r\"(?i)\\b(?:href|data-href|data-url|data-link|data-button-url|onclick)\\s*=\\s*([\\\"'])(.*?)\\1\",\n"
    "    re.S,\n"
    ")\n"
    "_CHANNEL_CATCHUP_KEY_V11220 = \"channel_catchup_v11220\"\n"
    "_CHANNEL_CATCHUP_MAX_PAGES_V11220 = 256\n"
    "_CHANNEL_CATCHUP_ATTEMPTS_V11220 = 3\n"
    "_LIVE_CHANNEL_HEADER_V11220 = re.compile(\n"
    "    r\"(?:🎬|📺)\\s*(?:(?:电影|剧集|电视剧|动漫|动画)\\s*[：:]\\s*)?([^\\n]{2,360})\",\n"
    "    re.I,\n"
    ")\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "def _normalize_channel_source_urls_v11221(values: List[str], legacy_module: Any) -> List[str]:\n"
    "    \"\"\"兼容根域名配置：tgm.li668.asia -> 默认频道路径。\"\"\"\n"
    "    defaults = list(getattr(legacy_module, \"DEFAULT_CHANNEL_URLS\", []) or [])\n"
    "    default_paths: List[str] = []\n"
    "    default_hosts = set()\n"
    "    for item in defaults:\n"
    "        raw = str(item or \"\").strip()\n"
    "        if not raw:\n"
    "            continue\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            parsed = urlsplit(raw)\n"
    "        except ValueError:\n"
    "            continue\n"
    "        host = (parsed.hostname or \"\").lower()\n"
    "        if host:\n"
    "            default_hosts.add(host)\n"
    "        path = str(parsed.path or \"\").strip()\n"
    "        if path and path != \"/\" and path not in default_paths:\n"
    "            default_paths.append(path.rstrip(\"/\"))\n"
    "\n"
    "    rows: List[str] = []\n"
    "    seen = set()\n"
    "    for item in values:\n"
    "        raw = html.unescape(str(item or \"\")).replace(\"\\\\/\", \"/\").strip()\n"
    "        if not raw:\n"
    "            continue\n"
    "        if not re.match(r\"(?i)^https?://\", raw):\n"
    "            raw = \"https://\" + raw.lstrip(\"/\")\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            parsed = urlsplit(raw)\n"
    "        except ValueError:\n"
    "            continue\n"
    "        host = (parsed.hostname or \"\").lower()\n"
    "        if not host:\n"
    "            continue\n"
    "        scheme = parsed.scheme or \"https\"\n"
    "        netloc = parsed.netloc\n"
    "        path = str(parsed.path or \"\").strip()\n"
    "        query = str(parsed.query or \"\").strip()\n"
    "        if path and path != \"/\":\n"
    "            normalized = urlunsplit((scheme, netloc, path.rstrip(\"/\"), query, \"\"))\n"
    "            if normalized not in seen:\n"
    "                seen.add(normalized)\n"
    "                rows.append(normalized)\n"
    "            continue\n"
    "        if host in default_hosts and default_paths:\n"
    "            for default_path in default_paths:\n"
    "                expanded = urlunsplit((scheme, netloc, default_path, query, \"\"))\n"
    "                if expanded in seen:\n"
    "                    continue\n"
    "                seen.add(expanded)\n"
    "                rows.append(expanded)\n"
    "            continue\n"
    "        normalized = urlunsplit((scheme, netloc, \"\", query, \"\"))\n"
    "        if normalized not in seen:\n"
    "            seen.add(normalized)\n"
    "            rows.append(normalized)\n"
    "    return rows\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "def _resource_group_id(source_url: str, message_id: str, text: str) -> str:\n"
    "    if message_id:\n"
    "        marker = f\"msg:{message_id}\"\n"
    "    else:\n"
    "        stable = re.sub(r\"\\s+\", \" \", str(text or \"\")).strip()\n"
    "        marker = \"txt:\" + hashlib.sha256(stable.encode(\"utf-8\")).hexdigest()[:20]\n"
    "    return hashlib.sha256(f\"{source_url}|{marker}\".encode(\"utf-8\")).hexdigest()[:24]\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "def _clean_external_uri(value: str) -> str:\n"
    "    value = html.unescape(str(value or \"\")).replace(\"\\\\/\", \"/\").strip()\n"
    "    return value.rstrip(\".,，。;；)）]】\")\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "def _better_episode_hint(text: Any, current: Any = \"\") -> str:\n"
    "    \"\"\"补足 legacy 旧模板对四位绝对集号/“话”格式的覆盖。\"\"\"\n"
    "    current = str(current or \"\").strip()\n"
    "    raw = str(text or \"\")\n"
    "    patterns = (\n"
    "        r\"(?i)S\\d{1,2}\\s*[._ -]*E\\d{1,4}(?:\\s*[-~～—至+]\\s*E?\\d{1,4})?\",\n"
    "        r\"(?i)(?:EP|Episode)[\\s._-]*\\d{1,4}(?:\\s*[-~～—至+]\\s*(?:EP|Episode)?\\s*\\d{1,4})?\",\n"
    "        r\"第\\s*\\d{1,4}\\s*[-~～—至]\\s*\\d{1,4}\\s*[集话]\",\n"
    "        r\"第\\s*\\d{1,4}\\s*[集话]\",\n"
    "        r\"(?:更新至|更新到|更至)\\s*(?:第\\s*)?\\d{1,4}\\s*[集话]?\",\n"
    "    )\n"
    "    for pattern in patterns:\n"
    "        matched = re.search(pattern, raw)\n"
    "        if matched:\n"
    "            candidate = matched.group(0).strip()\n"
    "            # legacy 已经给出更具体的 SxxExx 时继续保留；否则采用四位/话等增强结果。\n"
    "            if not current or len(re.findall(r\"\\d\", candidate)) > len(re.findall(r\"\\d\", current)) or \"话\" in candidate:\n"
    "                return candidate[:120]\n"
    "    return current[:120]\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "def _clean_live_channel_title_v11220(value: Any) -> str:\n"
    "    \"\"\"把频道模板字段剥离成作品标题；不做任何模糊标题改写。\"\"\"\n"
    "    title = html.unescape(str(value or \"\")).strip()\n"
    "    title = re.sub(r\"^[\\s🎬🎞🎥📺]+\", \"\", title).strip()\n"
    "    title = re.sub(r\"^(?:电影|剧集|电视剧|动漫|动画)\\s*[：:]\\s*\", \"\", title, flags=re.I).strip()\n"
    "    title = re.sub(r\"\\s+\", \" \", title)\n"
    "    title = re.sub(\n"
    "        r\"\\s*[（(]\\s*(?:19\\d{2}|20\\d{2})\\s*[）)]\"\n"
    "        r\"(?:\\s*S\\d{1,2}(?:\\s*[._ -]*E\\d{1,4}(?:\\s*[-~～—至+]\\s*E?\\d{1,4})?)?)?\"\n"
    "        r\"\\s*(?:已?更新|更新中|已?完结|完结|全集|全季)?\\s*$\",\n"
    "        \"\",\n"
    "        title,\n"
    "        flags=re.I,\n"
    "    ).strip()\n"
    "    title = re.sub(r\"\\s*(?:已?更新|更新中|已?完结|完结)\\s*$\", \"\", title, flags=re.I).strip()\n"
    "    return title[:300]\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "def _install_channel_title_compat_v11220(legacy_module: Any) -> None:\n"
    "    \"\"\"热重载安全地把当前 tgm 热更模板接入 legacy 标题解析。\"\"\"\n"
    "    current_clean = getattr(legacy_module, \"_clean_channel_display_title\", None)\n"
    "    current_extract = getattr(legacy_module, \"_extract_channel_display_title\", None)\n"
    "    if not callable(current_clean) or not callable(current_extract):\n"
    "        return\n"
    "    if getattr(current_extract, \"_guangya_live_channel_title_v11220\", False):\n"
    "        return\n"
    "\n"
    "    original_clean = current_clean\n"
    "    original_extract = current_extract\n"
    "\n"
    "    @functools.wraps(original_clean)\n"
    "    def patched_clean(value: Any) -> str:\n"
    "        # 先复用既有清洗，再补当前频道的类型前缀/季集尾巴；如果旧清洗未处理则直接处理原值。\n"
    "        cleaned = _clean_live_channel_title_v11220(original_clean(value))\n"
    "        if cleaned:\n"
    "            return cleaned\n"
    "        return _clean_live_channel_title_v11220(value)\n"
    "\n"
    "    legacy_module._clean_channel_display_title = patched_clean\n"
    "\n"
    "    @functools.wraps(original_extract)\n"
    "    def patched_extract_title(value: Any) -> str:\n"
    "        raw = str(value or \"\")\n"
    "        matched = _LIVE_CHANNEL_HEADER_V11220.search(raw)\n"
    "        if matched:\n"
    "            candidate = str(matched.group(1) or \"\")\n"
    "            boundary = getattr(legacy_module, \"_CHANNEL_META_BOUNDARY\", None)\n"
    "            if boundary is not None:\n"
    "                try:\n"
    "                    candidate = boundary.split(candidate, maxsplit=1)[0]\n"
    "                except Exception:\n"
    "                    pass\n"
    "            cleaned = patched_clean(candidate)\n"
    "            if cleaned:\n"
    "                return cleaned\n"
    "        return patched_clean(original_extract(value))\n"
    "\n"
    "    patched_extract_title._guangya_live_channel_title_v11220 = True\n"
    "    patched_extract_title._guangya_original_extract_channel_title = original_extract\n"
    "    legacy_module._extract_channel_display_title = patched_extract_title\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "def _cursor_snapshot_v11220(raw: Any) -> Dict[str, int]:\n"
    "    result: Dict[str, int] = {}\n"
    "    if not isinstance(raw, dict):\n"
    "        return result\n"
    "    for source, row in raw.items():\n"
    "        value = row.get(\"last_message_id\") if isinstance(row, dict) else row\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            cursor = int(value or 0)\n"
    "        except (TypeError, ValueError):\n"
    "            cursor = 0\n"
    "        result[str(source or \"\").strip()] = max(0, cursor)\n"
    "    return result\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "def _channel_label_v11220(source_url: str) -> str:\n"
    "    return \"光鸭云盘影视热更频道\" if \"regeng\" in str(source_url or \"\").lower() else \"光鸭云盘资源分享频道\"\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "def _install_channel_cursor_completeness_v11220(legacy_module: Any) -> None:\n"
    "    \"\"\"包住 legacy.refresh_channels：没追到旧游标就回滚游标，绝不越过未读消息。\"\"\"\n"
    "    assistant_cls = getattr(legacy_module, \"GuangYaTransferAssistant\", None)\n"
    "    current_refresh = getattr(assistant_cls, \"refresh_channels\", None) if assistant_cls else None\n"
    "    if not callable(current_refresh) or getattr(current_refresh, \"_guangya_channel_complete_v11220\", False):\n"
    "        return\n"
    "    original_refresh = current_refresh\n"
    "\n"
    "    @functools.wraps(original_refresh)\n"
    "    def patched_refresh(self, force: bool = False):\n"
    "        source_urls = list(self._source_urls() or [])\n"
    "        before_raw = self.get_data(\"channel_cursors\") or {}\n"
    "        before_cursors = _cursor_snapshot_v11220(before_raw)\n"
    "        catchup = self.get_data(_CHANNEL_CATCHUP_KEY_V11220) or {}\n"
    "        if not isinstance(catchup, dict):\n"
    "            catchup = {}\n"
    "        catchup = dict(catchup)\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            configured_pages = max(1, int(getattr(self, \"_history_pages\", 1) or 1))\n"
    "        except (TypeError, ValueError):\n"
    "            configured_pages = 1\n"
    "        active_budgets = []\n"
    "        for source_url in source_urls:\n"
    "            row = catchup.get(source_url) or {}\n"
    "            try:\n"
    "                active_budgets.append(int((row or {}).get(\"page_budget\") or 0))\n"
    "            except (TypeError, ValueError):\n"
    "                pass\n"
    "        page_budget = min(\n"
    "            _CHANNEL_CATCHUP_MAX_PAGES_V11220,\n"
    "            max([configured_pages, *active_budgets]),\n"
    "        )\n"
    "        rows: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []\n"
    "        incomplete: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]] = {}\n"
    "        last_index: Dict[str, Any] = {}\n"
    "\n"
    "        for attempt in range(_CHANNEL_CATCHUP_ATTEMPTS_V11220):\n"
    "            previous_limit = getattr(self, \"_history_pages\", configured_pages)\n"
    "            self._history_pages = page_budget\n"
    "            try:\n"
    "                rows = list(original_refresh(self, force=bool(force or catchup or attempt > 0)) or [])\n"
    "            finally:\n"
    "                self._history_pages = previous_limit\n"
    "\n"
    "            after_raw = self.get_data(\"channel_cursors\") or {}\n"
    "            after_cursors = _cursor_snapshot_v11220(after_raw)\n"
    "            last_index = dict(self.get_data(\"channel_index\") or {})\n"
    "            source_status = dict(last_index.get(\"source_status\") or {})\n"
    "            incomplete = {}\n"
    "            for source_url in source_urls:\n"
    "                old_cursor = int(before_cursors.get(source_url, 0) or 0)\n"
    "                new_cursor = int(after_cursors.get(source_url, 0) or 0)\n"
    "                status = dict(source_status.get(_channel_label_v11220(source_url)) or {})\n"
    "                if (\n"
    "                    old_cursor > 0\n"
    "                    and new_cursor > old_cursor\n"
    "                    and bool(status.get(\"success\"))\n"
    "                    and not bool(status.get(\"reached_cursor\"))\n"
    "                ):\n"
    "                    incomplete[source_url] = {\n"
    "                        \"old_cursor\": old_cursor,\n"
    "                        \"high_watermark\": new_cursor,\n"
    "                        \"status\": status,\n"
    "                    }\n"
    "\n"
    "            if not incomplete:\n"
    "                changed = False\n"
    "                for source_url in source_urls:\n"
    "                    status = dict(source_status.get(_channel_label_v11220(source_url)) or {})\n"
    "                    if bool(status.get(\"success\")) and source_url in catchup:\n"
    "                        catchup.pop(source_url, None)\n"
    "                        changed = True\n"
    "                    if status and status.get(\"catchup_incomplete\"):\n"
    "                        status[\"catchup_incomplete\"] = False\n"
    "                        status[\"page_budget\"] = page_budget\n"
    "                        source_status[_channel_label_v11220(source_url)] = status\n"
    "                        changed = True\n"
    "                if changed:\n"
    "                    catchup = {\n"
    "                        key: dict(value) for key, value in catchup.items()\n"
    "                        if key in set(source_urls) and isinstance(value, dict)\n"
    "                    }\n"
    "                    last_index[\"source_status\"] = source_status\n"
    "                    self.save_data(\"channel_index\", last_index)\n"
    "                    self.save_data(_CHANNEL_CATCHUP_KEY_V11220, catchup)\n"
    "                return rows\n"
    "\n"
    "            # legacy 已经把游标推进到当前抓取的最高 ID；在继续翻页前先恢复旧游标。\n"
    "            restored_cursors = dict(after_raw) if isinstance(after_raw, dict) else {}\n"
    "            for source_url, detail in incomplete.items():\n"
    "                old_row = dict((before_raw or {}).get(source_url) or {}) if isinstance((before_raw or {}).get(source_url), dict) else {}\n"
    "                old_row[\"last_message_id\"] = int(detail[\"old_cursor\"])\n"
    "                restored_cursors[source_url] = old_row\n"
    "            self.save_data(\"channel_cursors\", restored_cursors)\n"
    "\n"
    "            if attempt + 1 < _CHANNEL_CATCHUP_ATTEMPTS_V11220 and page_budget < _CHANNEL_CATCHUP_MAX_PAGES_V11220:\n"
    "                page_budget = min(\n"
    "                    _CHANNEL_CATCHUP_MAX_PAGES_V11220,\n"
    "                    max(configured_pages * 2, page_budget * 2),\n"
    "                )\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            break\n"
    "\n"
    "        # 本轮有界追赶仍未触达旧游标：保留旧游标，并为下轮继续扩大预算；这是延迟，不是静默丢失。\n"
    "        index = dict(self.get_data(\"channel_index\") or last_index or {})\n"
    "        source_status = dict(index.get(\"source_status\") or {})\n"
    "        next_budget = min(\n"
    "            _CHANNEL_CATCHUP_MAX_PAGES_V11220,\n"
    "            max(configured_pages * 2, page_budget * 2),\n"
    "        )\n"
    "        now_text = \"\"\n"
    "        now_reader = getattr(self, \"_now_text\", None)\n"
    "        if callable(now_reader):\n"
    "            try:\n"
    "                now_text = str(now_reader() or \"\")\n"
    "            except Exception:\n"
    "                now_text = \"\"\n"
    "        for source_url, detail in incomplete.items():\n"
    "            old_cursor = int(detail[\"old_cursor\"])\n"
    "            high_watermark = int(detail[\"high_watermark\"])\n"
    "            catchup[source_url] = {\n"
    "                \"last_message_id\": old_cursor,\n"
    "                \"high_watermark\": high_watermark,\n"
    "                \"page_budget\": next_budget,\n"
    "                \"updated\": now_text,\n"
    "            }\n"
    "            label = _channel_label_v11220(source_url)\n"
    "            status = dict(source_status.get(label) or detail.get(\"status\") or {})\n"
    "            status.update({\n"
    "                \"cursor\": old_cursor,\n"
    "                \"high_watermark\": high_watermark,\n"
    "                \"page_budget\": page_budget,\n"
    "                \"next_page_budget\": next_budget,\n"
    "                \"catchup_incomplete\": True,\n"
    "            })\n"
    "            source_status[label] = status\n"
    "            self._plugin_log(\n"
    "                \"WARNING\",\n"
    "                \"【光鸭转存助手】【频道完整性v1.12.20】%s 已抓到高水位 %s，但 %s 页预算仍未追到旧游标 %s；\"\n"
    "                \"已回退游标，下一轮页预算=%s，不会越过未读取消息\",\n"
    "                label,\n"
    "                high_watermark,\n"
    "                page_budget,\n"
    "                old_cursor,\n"
    "                next_budget,\n"
    "            )\n"
    "        catchup = {\n"
    "            key: dict(value) for key, value in catchup.items()\n"
    "            if key in set(source_urls) and isinstance(value, dict)\n"
    "        }\n"
    "        index[\"source_status\"] = source_status\n"
    "        self.save_data(\"channel_index\", index)\n"
    "        self.save_data(_CHANNEL_CATCHUP_KEY_V11220, catchup)\n"
    "        return rows\n"
    "\n"
    "    patched_refresh._guangya_channel_complete_v11220 = True\n"
    "    patched_refresh._guangya_original_refresh_channels = original_refresh\n"
    "    assistant_cls.refresh_channels = patched_refresh\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "def _channel_slug_from_url(source_url: str) -> str:\n"
    "    try:\n"
    "        path = str(urlsplit(str(source_url or \"\")).path or \"\").strip(\"/\")\n"
    "    except ValueError:\n"
    "        return str(source_url or \"\")[-40:]\n"
    "    return path.split(\"/\")[-1] if path else str(source_url or \"\")[-40:]\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "_CHANNEL_DECODE_MAX_CHARS = 2_000_000\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "def _decode_channel_blob(value: Any) -> str:\n"
    "    \"\"\"occurrence discovery 前规范化：HTML 实体 → \\\\/ / \\\\u002F → 有限次 unquote。\"\"\"\n"
    "    current = html.unescape(str(value or \"\"))\n"
    "    if len(current) > _CHANNEL_DECODE_MAX_CHARS:\n"
    "        current = current[:_CHANNEL_DECODE_MAX_CHARS]\n"
    "    current = current.replace(\"\\\\/\", \"/\").replace(\"\\\\u002F\", \"/\").replace(\"\\\\u002f\", \"/\")\n"
    "    for _ in range(3):\n"
    "        decoded = unquote(current)\n"
    "        if decoded == current:\n"
    "            break\n"
    "        current = decoded\n"
    "        if len(current) > _CHANNEL_DECODE_MAX_CHARS:\n"
    "            current = current[:_CHANNEL_DECODE_MAX_CHARS]\n"
    "            break\n"
    "    return current\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "def _external_sources_from_context(context_html: str) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:\n"
    "    # 先整段 decode，再 finditer；属性值同样先 decode\n"
    "    raw = str(context_html or \"\")\n"
    "    decoded = _decode_channel_blob(raw)\n"
    "    attr_bits = []\n"
    "    for matched in _ATTR_URL_RE.finditer(raw):\n"
    "        attr_bits.append(_decode_channel_blob(matched.group(2)))\n"
    "    if attr_bits:\n"
    "        decoded = decoded + \"\\n\" + \"\\n\".join(attr_bits)\n"
    "    rows: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []\n"
    "    seen = set()\n"
    "    matches = [(item.start(), item.group(0)) for item in _MAGNET_RE.finditer(decoded)]\n"
    "    matches.extend((item.start(), item.group(0)) for item in _ED2K_RE.finditer(decoded))\n"
    "    matches.sort(key=lambda pair: pair[0])\n"
    "    for _, raw in matches:\n"
    "        uri = _clean_external_uri(raw)\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            normalized = normalize_source_uri(uri)\n"
    "        except Exception:\n"
    "            continue\n"
    "        key = f\"{normalized.get('type')}:{normalized.get('identity')}\"\n"
    "        if key in seen:\n"
    "            continue\n"
    "        seen.add(key)\n"
    "        rows.append({\n"
    "            \"type\": str(normalized.get(\"type\") or \"\"),\n"
    "            \"uri\": uri,\n"
    "            \"identity\": str(normalized.get(\"identity\") or \"\"),\n"
    "            \"name\": str(normalized.get(\"name\") or \"\")[:300],\n"
    "            \"size\": int(normalized.get(\"size\") or 0),\n"
    "        })\n"
    "    return rows\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "def _log_resource_discovery(legacy_module: Any, source_url: str, entry: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n"
    "    \"\"\"频道级调试日志；不打印 cookie/token。\"\"\"\n"
    "    logger = getattr(legacy_module, \"logger\", None)\n"
    "    if logger is None:\n"
    "        return\n"
    "    channel = _channel_slug_from_url(source_url)\n"
    "    message_id = str(entry.get(\"message_id\") or \"\") or \"-\"\n"
    "    xunlei_n = len(entry.get(\"xunlei_sources\") or [])\n"
    "    guangya_n = 1 if str(entry.get(\"share_url\") or \"\").strip() else 0\n"
    "    magnet_n = sum(1 for item in (entry.get(\"external_sources\") or []) if str(item.get(\"type\") or \"\") == \"magnet\")\n"
    "    ed2k_n = sum(1 for item in (entry.get(\"external_sources\") or []) if str(item.get(\"type\") or \"\") == \"ed2k\")\n"
    "    try:\n"
    "        logger.info(\n"
    "            \"【光鸭转存助手】【频道】channel=%s message_id=%s\",\n"
    "            channel,\n"
    "            message_id,\n"
    "        )\n"
    "        logger.info(\n"
    "            \"【光鸭转存助手】【资源发现】xunlei=%s guangya=%s magnet=%s ed2k=%s\",\n"
    "            xunlei_n,\n"
    "            guangya_n,\n"
    "            magnet_n,\n"
    "            ed2k_n,\n"
    "        )\n"
    "        title = str(entry.get(\"display_title\") or entry.get(\"title\") or \"\")[:120]\n"
    "        if title or entry.get(\"episode_hint\"):\n"
    "            logger.info(\n"
    "                \"【光鸭转存助手】【媒体匹配】title=%s season=%s episodes=%s\",\n"
    "                title or \"-\",\n"
    "                entry.get(\"season_hint\") or entry.get(\"season\") or \"-\",\n"
    "                entry.get(\"episode_hint\") or \"-\",\n"
    "            )\n"
    "    except Exception:\n"
    "        return\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "def install_channel_multisource_compat(legacy_module: Any):\n"
    "    \"\"\"热重载安全地扩展频道解析器、消息稳定键和 v1.12.20 完整性补丁。\"\"\"\n"
    "    _install_channel_title_compat_v11220(legacy_module)\n"
    "    _install_channel_cursor_completeness_v11220(legacy_module)\n"
    "    assistant_cls = getattr(legacy_module, \"GuangYaTransferAssistant\", None)\n"
    "    current_source_urls = getattr(assistant_cls, \"_source_urls\", None) if assistant_cls else None\n"
    "    if callable(current_source_urls) and not getattr(current_source_urls, \"_guangya_source_urls_v11221\", False):\n"
    "        original_source_urls = current_source_urls\n"
    "\n"
    "        @functools.wraps(original_source_urls)\n"
    "        def patched_source_urls(self) -> List[str]:\n"
    "            original = [str(item or \"\").strip() for item in (original_source_urls(self) or []) if str(item or \"\").strip()]\n"
    "            normalized = _normalize_channel_source_urls_v11221(original, legacy_module)\n"
    "            if normalized:\n"
    "                return normalized\n"
    "            fallbacks = [str(item or \"\").strip() for item in (getattr(legacy_module, \"DEFAULT_CHANNEL_URLS\", []) or []) if str(item or \"\").strip()]\n"
    "            return fallbacks\n"
    "\n"
    "        patched_source_urls._guangya_source_urls_v11221 = True\n"
    "        patched_source_urls._guangya_original_source_urls = original_source_urls\n"
    "        assistant_cls._source_urls = patched_source_urls\n"
    "\n"
    "    current_extract = getattr(legacy_module, \"_extract_channel_entries\", None)\n"
    "    current_key = getattr(legacy_module, \"_entry_process_key\", None)\n"
    "    if not callable(current_extract) or not callable(current_key):\n"
    "        return None\n"
    "\n"
    "    if not getattr(current_key, \"_guangya_resource_group_key\", False):\n"
    "        original_key = current_key\n"
    "\n"
    "        @functools.wraps(original_key)\n"
    "        def patched_key(entry: Dict[str, Any]) -> str:\n"
    "            existing = original_key(entry)\n"
    "            if existing:\n"
    "                return existing\n"
    "            group_id = str((entry or {}).get(\"resource_group_id\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "            if not group_id:\n"
    "                return \"\"\n"
    "            return hashlib.sha256(f\"resource-group|{group_id}\".encode(\"utf-8\")).hexdigest()\n"
    "\n"
    "        patched_key._guangya_resource_group_key = True\n"
    "        patched_key._guangya_original_entry_process_key = original_key\n"
    "        legacy_module._entry_process_key = patched_key\n"
    "\n"
    "    current_extract = getattr(legacy_module, \"_extract_channel_entries\", None)\n"
    "    if getattr(current_extract, \"_guangya_channel_multisource\", False):\n"
    "        return current_extract\n"
    "    original_extract = current_extract\n"
    "\n"
    "    @functools.wraps(original_extract)\n"
    "    def patched_extract(page_text: str, source_url: str, source_label: str) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:\n"
    "        base_entries = list(original_extract(page_text, source_url, source_label) or [])\n"
    "        # 必须在 Magnet/ED2K occurrence discovery 之前完成 URL decode\n"
    "        decoded = _decode_channel_blob(page_text)\n"
    "        occurrence_positions = [item.start() for item in _MAGNET_RE.finditer(decoded)]\n"
    "        occurrence_positions.extend(item.start() for item in _ED2K_RE.finditer(decoded))\n"
    "\n"
    "        groups: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]] = {}\n"
    "        for position in sorted(set(occurrence_positions)):\n"
    "            context_html = legacy_module._message_context_html(decoded, position)\n"
    "            context = legacy_module._html_to_text(context_html)\n"
    "            external = _external_sources_from_context(context_html)\n"
    "            if not external:\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            metadata = dict(legacy_module._entry_metadata(context, context_html) or {})\n"
    "            metadata[\"episode_hint\"] = _better_episode_hint(context, metadata.get(\"episode_hint\"))\n"
    "            message_id = str(metadata.get(\"message_id\") or \"\")\n"
    "            group_id = _resource_group_id(source_url, message_id, context)\n"
    "            row = groups.get(group_id)\n"
    "            if row is None:\n"
    "                row = {\n"
    "                    \"resource_group_id\": group_id,\n"
    "                    \"text\": context[:4000],\n"
    "                    \"source_url\": source_url,\n"
    "                    \"source_label\": source_label,\n"
    "                    \"priority\": 0 if \"regeng\" in source_url.lower() else 1,\n"
    "                    \"stale\": False,\n"
    "                    \"cached_index\": False,\n"
    "                    \"external_sources\": [],\n"
    "                    **metadata,\n"
    "                }\n"
    "                groups[group_id] = row\n"
    "            seen = {f\"{item.get('type')}:{item.get('identity')}\" for item in row[\"external_sources\"]}\n"
    "            for item in external:\n"
    "                key = f\"{item.get('type')}:{item.get('identity')}\"\n"
    "                if key not in seen:\n"
    "                    seen.add(key)\n"
    "                    row[\"external_sources\"].append(item)\n"
    "\n"
    "        attached_groups = set()\n"
    "        for entry in base_entries:\n"
    "            entry[\"episode_hint\"] = _better_episode_hint(entry.get(\"text\"), entry.get(\"episode_hint\"))\n"
    "            message_id = str(entry.get(\"message_id\") or \"\")\n"
    "            candidates = []\n"
    "            for group_id, group in groups.items():\n"
    "                same_message = bool(message_id and message_id == str(group.get(\"message_id\") or \"\"))\n"
    "                if not same_message and not message_id and str(entry.get(\"text\") or \"\") == str(group.get(\"text\") or \"\"):\n"
    "                    same_message = True\n"
    "                if not same_message:\n"
    "                    continue\n"
    "                entry[\"resource_group_id\"] = group_id\n"
    "                entry[\"external_sources\"] = list(group.get(\"external_sources\") or [])\n"
    "                entry[\"candidate_types\"] = [\"guangya\", *[str(item.get(\"type\") or \"\") for item in entry[\"external_sources\"]]]\n"
    "                attached_groups.add(group_id)\n"
    "                candidates = entry[\"external_sources\"]\n"
    "                break\n"
    "            if not candidates:\n"
    "                group_id = _resource_group_id(source_url, message_id, str(entry.get(\"text\") or \"\"))\n"
    "                entry[\"resource_group_id\"] = group_id\n"
    "                entry.setdefault(\"external_sources\", [])\n"
    "                entry[\"candidate_types\"] = [\"guangya\"]\n"
    "\n"
    "        for group_id, group in groups.items():\n"
    "            if group_id in attached_groups:\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            pseudo = {\n"
    "                **group,\n"
    "                \"share_url\": \"\",\n"
    "                \"share_id\": \"\",\n"
    "                \"link_style\": \"外部资源\",\n"
    "                \"candidate_types\": [str(item.get(\"type\") or \"\") for item in group.get(\"external_sources\") or []],\n"
    "            }\n"
    "            base_entries.append(pseudo)\n"
    "\n"
    "        for entry in base_entries:\n"
    "            if entry.get(\"external_sources\") or entry.get(\"share_url\") or entry.get(\"xunlei_sources\"):\n"
    "                _log_resource_discovery(legacy_module, source_url, entry)\n"
    "\n"
    "        return base_entries\n"
    "\n"
    "    patched_extract._guangya_channel_multisource = True\n"
    "    patched_extract._guangya_original_extract_channel_entries = original_extract\n"
    "    legacy_module._extract_channel_entries = patched_extract\n"
    "    return patched_extract\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "__all__ = [\n"
    "    \"install_channel_multisource_compat\",\n"
    "]\n"
    "\n"
)

# ===== offline_safety_v180.py =====
_INLINE_MODULE_SOURCES["offline_safety_v180"] = (
    "\"\"\"v1.8.0 光鸭原生云添加安全收口。\n"
    "\n"
    "该层只修正任务恢复和对外展示，不改变 multisource_v180 的业务入口：\n"
    "- 有 taskId 的 queued/submitted/waiting 永远轮询，不重复 create_task；\n"
    "- 已提交任务的临时轮询网络错误不会把任务误判为永久失败；\n"
    "- 来源列表不返回 Magnet tracker 等原始 URI，避免把可能含私有参数的链接暴露到状态接口。\n"
    "\"\"\"\n"
    "\n"
    "from __future__ import annotations\n"
    "\n"
    "from typing import Any, Dict\n"
    "\n"
    "from .source_types_v180 import SOURCE_INFLIGHT_STATES\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "class GuangYaOfflineSafetyMixin:\n"
    "    \"\"\"必须放在 GuangYaMultiSourceMixin 之前。\"\"\"\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _submit_offline_source(self, source_id: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        source = dict(self._source_store()[\"items\"].get(str(source_id or \"\")) or {})\n"
    "        task_id = str(source.get(\"task_id\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "        state = str(source.get(\"state\") or \"new\")\n"
    "        if task_id:\n"
    "            if state == \"completed\":\n"
    "                return {\"success\": True, \"message\": \"来源已完成\", \"data\": source}\n"
    "            if state in {\"retry\", \"failed\"}:\n"
    "                return self._retry_offline_task(source)\n"
    "            # 只要服务端任务已经存在，不论旧版本落下什么中间 state，都只能查询既有任务。\n"
    "            return self._poll_offline_source(source)\n"
    "        return super()._submit_offline_source(source_id)\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _poll_offline_source(self, source: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        before_attempts = int(source.get(\"attempts\") or 0)\n"
    "        result = super()._poll_offline_source(source)\n"
    "        data = result.get(\"data\") if isinstance(result, dict) else None\n"
    "        if not isinstance(data, dict):\n"
    "            return result\n"
    "\n"
    "        task_status = data.get(\"task_status\")\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            task_status_code = int(task_status if task_status is not None else -1)\n"
    "        except (TypeError, ValueError):\n"
    "            task_status_code = -1\n"
    "\n"
    "        # status=5 是光鸭明确返回的部分完成/失败，允许进入 retry/failed。\n"
    "        # 其它失败若已有 taskId，则属于“查询暂不可用”，必须保留任务等待下轮，不得重新提交。\n"
    "        if (\n"
    "            str(source.get(\"task_id\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "            and not bool(result.get(\"success\"))\n"
    "            and task_status_code != 5\n"
    "        ):\n"
    "            updated = self._update_source(\n"
    "                str(source.get(\"id\") or \"\"),\n"
    "                state=\"waiting\",\n"
    "                attempts=before_attempts,\n"
    "                next_retry_at=0,\n"
    "                last_error=str(result.get(\"message\") or data.get(\"last_error\") or \"任务状态查询暂不可用\")[:500],\n"
    "            ) or data\n"
    "            return {\n"
    "                \"success\": False,\n"
    "                \"message\": str(result.get(\"message\") or \"任务状态查询暂不可用，已保留 taskId 等待恢复\"),\n"
    "                \"data\": updated,\n"
    "            }\n"
    "        return result\n"
    "\n"
    "    @staticmethod\n"
    "    def _source_public_view(source: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        row = dict(source or {})\n"
    "        raw_uri = str(row.pop(\"uri\", \"\") or \"\")\n"
    "        source_type = str(row.get(\"type\") or \"\")\n"
    "        identity = str(row.get(\"identity\") or \"\")\n"
    "        name = str(row.get(\"name\") or row.get(\"resolved_name\") or \"\")[:120]\n"
    "        size = int(row.get(\"size\") or 0)\n"
    "        if source_type == \"magnet\":\n"
    "            row[\"uri_preview\"] = f\"magnet:?xt=urn:btih:{identity}\"\n"
    "        elif source_type == \"ed2k\":\n"
    "            row[\"uri_preview\"] = f\"ed2k://|file|{name}|{size}|{identity}|/\"\n"
    "        else:\n"
    "            row[\"uri_preview\"] = raw_uri[:120]\n"
    "        return row\n"
    "\n"
    "    def api_source_list(self, subscribe_id: int = 0) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        sid = int(subscribe_id or 0)\n"
    "        rows = []\n"
    "        for source in self._source_store()[\"items\"].values():\n"
    "            if not isinstance(source, dict):\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            if sid and int(source.get(\"subscribe_id\") or 0) != sid:\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            rows.append(self._source_public_view(source))\n"
    "        rows.sort(key=lambda row: str(row.get(\"updated_at\") or \"\"), reverse=True)\n"
    "        return {\"success\": True, \"count\": len(rows), \"data\": rows[:100]}\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "__all__ = [\"GuangYaOfflineSafetyMixin\"]\n"
    "\n"
)

# ===== source_store_v180.py =====
_INLINE_MODULE_SOURCES["source_store_v180"] = (
    "\"\"\"多来源订阅的配置与持久化。\n"
    "\n"
    "Magnet/ED2K 不是交给 MoviePilot 下载器的下载任务，而是光鸭云盘原生“云添加”来源。\n"
    "本层只负责绑定、去重、状态持久化和用户配置；具体 resolve/create/list 调度由\n"
    "multisource_v180 执行，v1.9.0 ResourceGroup 层负责同资源候选决策。\n"
    "\"\"\"\n"
    "\n"
    "from __future__ import annotations\n"
    "\n"
    "import time\n"
    "from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional\n"
    "\n"
    "from .source_types_v180 import (\n"
    "    SOURCE_SCHEMA_VERSION,\n"
    "    SOURCE_TYPES,\n"
    "    normalize_source_uri,\n"
    "    safe_int,\n"
    "    source_identity,\n"
    ")\n"
    "\n"
    "\n"
    "class GuangYaSourceStoreMixin:\n"
    "    \"\"\"保存 Magnet/ED2K 与 MoviePilot 订阅的绑定关系。\"\"\"\n"
    "\n"
    "    _external_auto_dispatch = True\n"
    "    _source_priority = (\"guangya\", \"magnet\", \"ed2k\")\n"
    "    _offline_poll_minutes = 2\n"
    "    _offline_retry_minutes = 15\n"
    "    _offline_max_attempts = 3\n"
    "\n"
    "    def init_plugin(self, config: dict = None) -> None:\n"
    "        config = dict(config or {})\n"
    "        self._external_auto_dispatch = bool(config.get(\"external_auto_dispatch\", True))\n"
    "\n"
    "        raw_priority = config.get(\"source_priority\") or \"guangya,magnet,ed2k\"\n"
    "        if isinstance(raw_priority, (list, tuple)):\n"
    "            tokens = [str(value).strip().lower() for value in raw_priority]\n"
    "        else:\n"
    "            tokens = [\n"
    "                value.strip().lower()\n"
    "                for value in str(raw_priority).replace(\"，\", \",\").split(\",\")\n"
    "            ]\n"
    "        ordered = []\n"
    "        for value in tokens:\n"
    "            if value in SOURCE_TYPES and value not in ordered:\n"
    "                ordered.append(value)\n"
    "        self._source_priority = tuple(ordered or (\"guangya\", \"magnet\", \"ed2k\"))\n"
    "\n"
    "        self._offline_poll_minutes = max(\n"
    "            1, min(safe_int(config.get(\"offline_poll_minutes\"), 2, 1), 60)\n"
    "        )\n"
    "        self._offline_retry_minutes = max(\n"
    "            1, min(safe_int(config.get(\"offline_retry_minutes\"), 15, 1), 720)\n"
    "        )\n"
    "        self._offline_max_attempts = max(\n"
    "            1, min(safe_int(config.get(\"offline_max_attempts\"), 3, 1), 20)\n"
    "        )\n"
    "        self._ensure_source_store()\n"
    "        super().init_plugin(config)\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _source_store(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        raw = self.get_data(\"subscription_sources\") or {}\n"
    "        if not isinstance(raw, dict):\n"
    "            raw = {}\n"
    "        items = raw.get(\"items\")\n"
    "        if not isinstance(items, dict):\n"
    "            items = {}\n"
    "        return {\n"
    "            \"schema\": SOURCE_SCHEMA_VERSION,\n"
    "            \"items\": {\n"
    "                str(key): dict(value)\n"
    "                for key, value in items.items()\n"
    "                if isinstance(value, dict)\n"
    "            },\n"
    "            \"updated_at\": str(raw.get(\"updated_at\") or \"\"),\n"
    "        }\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _ensure_source_store(self) -> None:\n"
    "        store = self._source_store()\n"
    "        self._save_source_store(store)\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _save_source_store(self, store: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n"
    "        store = dict(store or {})\n"
    "        store[\"schema\"] = SOURCE_SCHEMA_VERSION\n"
    "        store[\"updated_at\"] = (\n"
    "            self._now_text()\n"
    "            if hasattr(self, \"_now_text\")\n"
    "            else time.strftime(\"%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S\")\n"
    "        )\n"
    "        if not isinstance(store.get(\"items\"), dict):\n"
    "            store[\"items\"] = {}\n"
    "        self.save_data(\"subscription_sources\", store)\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _sources_for_subscription(\n"
    "        self,\n"
    "        subscribe_id: int,\n"
    "        *,\n"
    "        enabled_only: bool = True,\n"
    "    ) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:\n"
    "        sid = int(subscribe_id or 0)\n"
    "        rows: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []\n"
    "        for source_id, raw in self._source_store()[\"items\"].items():\n"
    "            if int(raw.get(\"subscribe_id\") or 0) != sid:\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            if enabled_only and not bool(raw.get(\"enabled\", True)):\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            row = dict(raw)\n"
    "            row[\"id\"] = str(raw.get(\"id\") or source_id)\n"
    "            rows.append(row)\n"
    "        priority = {name: index for index, name in enumerate(self._source_priority)}\n"
    "        rows.sort(\n"
    "            key=lambda row: (\n"
    "                priority.get(str(row.get(\"type\") or \"\"), 99),\n"
    "                str(row.get(\"created_at\") or \"\"),\n"
    "                str(row.get(\"id\") or \"\"),\n"
    "            )\n"
    "        )\n"
    "        return rows\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _upsert_source(\n"
    "        self,\n"
    "        subscribe_id: int,\n"
    "        uri: str,\n"
    "        *,\n"
    "        label: str = \"\",\n"
    "        origin: str = \"manual\",\n"
    "        enabled: bool = True,\n"
    "        auto_dispatch: Optional[bool] = None,\n"
    "    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        sid = int(subscribe_id or 0)\n"
    "        subscribe = self._find_subscription(sid) if sid else None\n"
    "        if not sid or not subscribe:\n"
    "            raise ValueError(\"MoviePilot 订阅不存在\")\n"
    "\n"
    "        normalized = normalize_source_uri(uri)\n"
    "        source_id = source_identity(normalized[\"type\"], normalized[\"identity\"], sid)\n"
    "        store = self._source_store()\n"
    "        previous = dict(store[\"items\"].get(source_id) or {})\n"
    "        now = self._now_text()\n"
    "        row = {\n"
    "            **previous,\n"
    "            **normalized,\n"
    "            \"id\": source_id,\n"
    "            \"subscribe_id\": sid,\n"
    "            \"label\": str(label or previous.get(\"label\") or \"\").strip()[:120],\n"
    "            \"origin\": str(origin or previous.get(\"origin\") or \"manual\").strip()[:40],\n"
    "            \"enabled\": bool(enabled),\n"
    "            \"state\": str(previous.get(\"state\") or \"new\"),\n"
    "            \"attempts\": int(previous.get(\"attempts\") or 0),\n"
    "            \"created_at\": str(previous.get(\"created_at\") or now),\n"
    "            \"updated_at\": now,\n"
    "            \"last_error\": str(previous.get(\"last_error\") or \"\"),\n"
    "            \"task_id\": str(previous.get(\"task_id\") or \"\"),\n"
    "            \"task_status\": previous.get(\"task_status\"),\n"
    "            \"progress\": int(previous.get(\"progress\") or 0),\n"
    "            \"file_id\": str(previous.get(\"file_id\") or \"\"),\n"
    "            \"resolved_name\": str(previous.get(\"resolved_name\") or \"\"),\n"
    "            \"resolved_url\": str(previous.get(\"resolved_url\") or \"\"),\n"
    "            \"selected_indexes\": list(previous.get(\"selected_indexes\") or []),\n"
    "            \"next_retry_at\": float(previous.get(\"next_retry_at\") or 0),\n"
    "            \"auto_dispatch\": (\n"
    "                self._external_auto_dispatch\n"
    "                if auto_dispatch is None\n"
    "                else bool(auto_dispatch)\n"
    "            ),\n"
    "        }\n"
    "        if not previous:\n"
    "            row[\"state\"] = \"new\"\n"
    "\n"
    "        store[\"items\"][source_id] = row\n"
    "        self._save_source_store(store)\n"
    "\n"
    "        # 外部来源一旦绑定，沿用现有固定分流硬门禁；绝不让原生 RSS/搜索重复下载。\n"
    "        self._add_selected_subscription(sid, persist=True)\n"
    "        self._record_route_health(\n"
    "            last_source_added_at=now,\n"
    "            last_source_added_type=row[\"type\"],\n"
    "            last_source_added_id=source_id,\n"
    "        )\n"
    "        return row\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _update_source(self, source_id: str, **fields: Any) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:\n"
    "        source_id = str(source_id or \"\").strip()\n"
    "        store = self._source_store()\n"
    "        row = store[\"items\"].get(source_id)\n"
    "        if not isinstance(row, dict):\n"
    "            return None\n"
    "        updated = dict(row)\n"
    "        updated.update(fields)\n"
    "        updated[\"updated_at\"] = self._now_text()\n"
    "        store[\"items\"][source_id] = updated\n"
    "        self._save_source_store(store)\n"
    "        return updated\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _delete_source(self, source_id: str) -> bool:\n"
    "        source_id = str(source_id or \"\").strip()\n"
    "        store = self._source_store()\n"
    "        row = store[\"items\"].pop(source_id, None)\n"
    "        if not row:\n"
    "            return False\n"
    "        self._save_source_store(store)\n"
    "        # 不自动移除固定路线：该订阅可能原本就是光鸭频道路线。\n"
    "        self._record_route_health(\n"
    "            last_source_deleted_at=self._now_text(),\n"
    "            last_source_deleted_id=source_id,\n"
    "        )\n"
    "        return True\n"
    "\n"
    "    def get_form(self):\n"
    "        form, defaults = super().get_form()\n"
    "        try:\n"
    "            content = form[0].get(\"content\") if form else None\n"
    "            if isinstance(content, list):\n"
    "                content.append({\n"
    "                    \"component\": \"VCard\",\n"
    "                    \"props\": {\"variant\": \"tonal\", \"class\": \"mt-3\"},\n"
    "                    \"content\": [\n"
    "                        {\n"
    "                            \"component\": \"VCardTitle\",\n"
    "                            \"text\": \"多来源订阅 · 光鸭原生云添加\",\n"
    "                        },\n"
    "                        {\n"
    "                            \"component\": \"VCardText\",\n"
    "                            \"text\": (\n"
    "                                \"Magnet 与 ED2K 都直接提交光鸭云盘自带的云添加/离线任务，\"\n"
    "                                \"不会经过 qBittorrent、Transmission、Aria2 或任何外部 Bridge。\"\n"
    "                                \"同等满足订阅规则时默认光鸭直接转存优先，其次 Magnet，最后 ED2K。\"\n"
    "                            ),\n"
    "                        },\n"
    "                        {\n"
    "                            \"component\": \"VRow\",\n"
    "                            \"content\": [\n"
    "                                {\n"
    "                                    \"component\": \"VCol\",\n"
    "                                    \"props\": {\"cols\": 12, \"md\": 3},\n"
    "                                    \"content\": [{\n"
    "                                        \"component\": \"VSwitch\",\n"
    "                                        \"props\": {\n"
    "                                            \"model\": \"external_auto_dispatch\",\n"
    "                                            \"label\": \"新增来源自动云添加\",\n"
    "                                        },\n"
    "                                    }],\n"
    "                                },\n"
    "                                {\n"
    "                                    \"component\": \"VCol\",\n"
    "                                    \"props\": {\"cols\": 12, \"md\": 5},\n"
    "                                    \"content\": [{\n"
    "                                        \"component\": \"VTextField\",\n"
    "                                        \"props\": {\n"
    "                                            \"model\": \"source_priority\",\n"
    "                                            \"label\": \"来源优先级\",\n"
    "                                            \"hint\": \"逗号分隔，默认 guangya,magnet,ed2k\",\n"
    "                                            \"persistent-hint\": True,\n"
    "                                        },\n"
    "                                    }],\n"
    "                                },\n"
    "                                {\n"
    "                                    \"component\": \"VCol\",\n"
    "                                    \"props\": {\"cols\": 6, \"md\": 2},\n"

    "                                    \"component\": \"VCol\",\n"

    "        except Exception:\n"
    "            pass\n"
    "\n"
    "        defaults.update({\n"
    "            \"external_auto_dispatch\": self._external_auto_dispatch,\n"
    "            \"source_priority\": \",\".join(self._source_priority),\n"
    "            \"offline_poll_minutes\": self._offline_poll_minutes,\n"
    "            \"offline_retry_minutes\": self._offline_retry_minutes,\n"

    "        })\n"
    "        return form, defaults\n"
    "\n"

    "        try:\n"
    "            return int(value or 0)\n"
    "        except (TypeError, ValueError):\n"
    "            return int(default)\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _status_source_rows_v191(self) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:\n"
    "        rows = [dict(row) for row in self._source_store()[\"items\"].values() if isinstance(row, dict)]\n"
    "        rows.sort(key=lambda row: str(row.get(\"updated_at\") or \"\"), reverse=True)\n"
    "        return rows\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _status_transfer_rows_v191(self) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:\n"
    "        rows = []\n"
    "        for key, raw in (self.get_data(\"transfer_jobs\") or {}).items():\n"
    "            if not isinstance(raw, dict):\n"
    "                continue\n"
    "            row = dict(raw)\n"
    "            row[\"_key\"] = str(key)\n"
    "            rows.append(row)\n"
    "        rows.sort(\n"
    "            key=lambda row: str(row.get(\"updated_at\") or row.get(\"time\") or row.get(\"submitted_at\") or \"\"),\n"
    "            reverse=True,\n"
    "        )\n"
    "        return rows\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _status_overview_v191(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:\n"
    "        report = dict(self._build_selfcheck() or {})\n"
    "        sources = self._status_source_rows_v191()\n"
    "        transfer_rows = self._status_transfer_rows_v191()\n"
    "        index = self.get_data(\"channel_index\") or {}\n"
    "        last_run = self.get_data(\"last_run\") or {}\n"
    "        plans = list((self.api_resource_plan().get(\"data\") or []))\n"
    "\n"
    "        source_summary = {\n"

    "            \"ed2k\": 0,\n"
    "            \"pending\": 0,\n"
    "            \"running\": 0,\n"
    "            \"completed\": 0,\n"
    "            \"failed\": 0,\n"
    "            \"review\": 0,\n"
    "        }\n"
    "        for row in sources:\n"
    "            source_type = str(row.get(\"type\") or \"\").lower()\n"
    "            if source_type in {\"magnet\", \"ed2k\"}:\n"
    "                source_summary[source_type] += 1\n"
    "            state = str(row.get(\"state\") or \"new\")\n"
    "            if state in SOURCE_PENDING_STATES:\n"
    "                source_summary[\"pending\"] += 1\n"
    "            elif state in SOURCE_INFLIGHT_STATES:\n"
    "                source_summary[\"running\"] += 1\n"
    "            elif state == \"completed\":\n"
    "                source_summary[\"completed\"] += 1\n"
    "            elif state == \"failed\":\n"
    "                source_summary[\"failed\"] += 1\n"
    "            elif state == \"needs_review\":\n"
    "                source_summary[\"review\"] += 1\n"
    "\n"
    "        critical_checks = [\n"
    "            dict(item) for item in (report.get(\"checks\") or [])\n"
    "            if isinstance(item, dict) and item.get(\"critical\") and not item.get(\"ok\")\n"
    "        ]\n"
    "        warning_checks = [\n"
    "            dict(item) for item in (report.get(\"checks\") or [])\n"
    "            if isinstance(item, dict) and not item.get(\"critical\") and not item.get(\"ok\")\n"
    "        ]\n"
    "\n"
    "        attention_sources = [\n"
    "            row for row in sources\n"
    "            if str(row.get(\"state\") or \"\") in {\"failed\", \"needs_review\"}\n"
    "        ]\n"
    "        active_sources = [\n"
    "            row for row in sources\n"
    "            if str(row.get(\"state\") or \"new\") in SOURCE_PENDING_STATES | SOURCE_INFLIGHT_STATES\n"
    "        ]\n"
    "        active_transfer_rows = [\n"
    "            row for row in transfer_rows\n"
    "            if str(row.get(\"status\") or \"\") in _TRANSFER_ACTIVE_STATES\n"
    "        ]\n"
    "        failed_transfer_rows = [\n"
    "            row for row in transfer_rows\n"
    "            if str(row.get(\"status\") or \"\") == \"failed\"\n"
    "        ]\n"
    "        unresolved_plans = [row for row in plans if row.get(\"uncovered\")]\n"
    "\n"
    "        attention_count = len(critical_checks) + len(attention_sources) + len(failed_transfer_rows)\n"
    "        overall = \"healthy\"\n"
    "        if critical_checks:\n"
    "            overall = \"error\"\n"
    "        elif attention_count:\n"
    "            overall = \"warning\"\n"
    "\n"
    "        overview = {\n"
    "            \"overall\": overall,\n"
    "            \"healthy\": not bool(critical_checks),\n"
    "            \"attention_count\": attention_count,\n"

    "            \"channel_count\": len(index.get(\"items\") or []),\n"

    "    def _source_title(self, row: Dict[str, Any]) -> str:\n"
    "        sid = self._safe_int(row.get(\"subscribe_id\"))\n"
    "        subscribe = self._find_subscription(sid) if sid else None\n"
    "        name = str(getattr(subscribe, \"name\", \"\") or row.get(\"resolved_name\") or row.get(\"name\") or \"未命名资源\")\n"
    "        source_type = str(row.get(\"type\") or \"source\").upper()\n"
    "        return f\"{source_type} · {name}\"\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _transfer_title(self, row: Dict[str, Any]) -> str:\n"
    "        sid = self._safe_int(row.get(\"subscribe_id\") or row.get(\"sid\"))\n"
    "        subscribe = self._find_subscription(sid) if sid else None\n"
    "        name = str(getattr(subscribe, \"name\", \"\") or row.get(\"name\") or \"光鸭转存任务\")\n"
    "        return f\"光鸭转存 · {name}\"\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _source_trace(self, row: Dict[str, Any]) -> str:\n"
    "        source_id = str(row.get(\"id\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "        task_id = str(row.get(\"task_id\") or \"\").strip()\n"
    "        trace = f\"追踪 source #{source_id[:16] or '-'}\"\n"
    "        if task_id:\n"
    "            trace += f\" · task {task_id[:20]}\"\n"
    "        return trace\n"
    "\n"
    "    @staticmethod\n"
    "    def _source_action_hint(state: str, row: Dict[str, Any] | None = None) -> str:\n"
    "        error = str((row or {}).get(\"last_error\") or \"\")\n"
    "        if state == \"needs_review\" and \"REMOTE_VERIFY_TIMEOUT\" in error:\n"
    "            return \"建议：先刷新云任务并确认目标目录/媒体库；若仍缺失，可让后续候选继续或手动重试该来源。\"\n"
    "        if state == \"needs_review\":\n"
    "            return \"建议：核对集号后再执行来源重试。\"\n"
    "        if state == \"failed\":\n"
    "            return \"建议：先刷新云任务确认最新状态，再执行来源重试。\"\n"
    "        return \"\"\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _attention_cards(self, overview: Dict[str, Any]) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:\n"
    "        cards: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []\n"
    "        for item in overview.get(\"critical_checks\") or []:\n"
    "            cards.append({\n"
    "                \"component\": \"VAlert\",\n"
    "                \"props\": {\n"
    "                    \"type\": \"error\",\n"
    "                    \"variant\": \"tonal\",\n"
    "                    \"density\": \"compact\",\n"
    "                    \"class\": \"mb-2\",\n"
    "                    \"title\": str(item.get(\"label\") or \"关键检查失败\"),\n"
    "                    \"text\": str(item.get(\"detail\") or \"需要检查插件运行状态\"),\n"
    "                },\n"
    "            })\n"
    "\n"
    "        for row in overview.get(\"attention_sources\") or []:\n"
    "            state = str(row.get(\"state\") or \"\")\n"
    "            error = str(row.get(\"last_error\") or \"\")\n"
    "            detail = error or (\"集号置信度不足，已停止自动拆包\" if state == \"needs_review\" else \"云添加任务失败\")\n"
    "            hint = self._source_action_hint(state, row)\n"
    "            text = f\"{_SOURCE_STATE_TEXT.get(state, state)} · {detail}\\n（{self._source_trace(row)}）\"\n"
    "            if hint:\n"
    "                text += f\"\\n{hint}\"\n"
    "            cards.append({\n"
    "                \"component\": \"VAlert\",\n"
    "                \"props\": {\n"
    "                    \"type\": \"warning\" if state == \"needs_review\" else \"error\",\n"
    "                    \"variant\": \"tonal\",\n"
    "                    \"density\": \"compact\",\n"
    "                    \"class\": \"mb-2\",\n"
    "                    \"title\": self._source_title(row),\n"
    "                    \"text\": text,\n"
    "                },\n"
    "            })\n"
    "\n"
    "        for row in overview.get(\"failed_transfer_rows\") or []:\n"
    "            if len(cards) >= 8:\n"
    "                break\n"
    "            error = str(row.get(\"error\") or row.get(\"message\") or \"转存任务失败\")[:300]\n"
    "            cards.append({\n"
    "                \"component\": \"VAlert\",\n"
    "                \"props\": {\n"
    "                    \"type\": \"error\",\n"
    "                    \"variant\": \"tonal\",\n"
    "                    \"density\": \"compact\",\n"
    "                    \"class\": \"mb-2\",\n"
    "                    \"title\": self._transfer_title(row),\n"
    "                    \"text\": error,\n"
    "                },\n"
    "            })\n"
    "        return cards[:8]\n"
    "\n"
    "    def _active_cards(self, overview: Dict[str, Any]) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:\n"
    "        cards: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []\n"
    "\n"
    "        for row in overview.get(\"active_transfer_rows\") or []:\n"
    "            cards.append({\n"
    "                \"component\": \"VAlert\",\n"
    "                \"props\": {\n"
    "                    \"type\": \"info\",\n"
    "                    \"variant\": \"tonal\",\n"
    "                    \"density\": \"compact\",\n"
    "                    \"class\": \"mb-2\",\n"
    "                    \"title\": self._transfer_title(row),\n"
    "                    \"text\": \"已提交到光鸭，等待目标文件落盘确认\",\n"
    "                },\n"
    "            })\n"
    "            if len(cards) >= 6:\n"
    "                return cards\n"
    "\n"
    "        for row in overview.get(\"active_sources\") or []:\n"
    "            state = str(row.get(\"state\") or \"new\")\n"
    "            progress = max(0, min(100, self._safe_int(row.get(\"progress\"))))\n"
    "            episodes = row.get(\"resolved_episodes\") or row.get(\"target_episodes\") or []\n"
    "            episode_text = \", \".join(f\"E{self._safe_int(value):02d}\" for value in episodes[:12])\n"
    "            error = str(row.get(\"last_error\") or \"\")\n"
    "            if state == \"waiting\" and error.startswith(\"PENDING_VERIFY:\"):\n"
    "                try:\n"
    "                    since = float(row.get(\"pending_verify_since\") or 0)\n"
    "                except (TypeError, ValueError):\n"
    "                    since = 0.0\n"
    "                waited = max(0, int(time.time() - since)) if since > 0 else 0\n"
    "                if waited >= 60:\n"
    "                    wait_text = f\" · 已等待 {waited // 60} 分钟\"\n"
    "                else:\n"
    "                    wait_text = \" · 刚进入核验\"\n"
    "                detail = f\"远端任务已完成，正在核验正片{wait_text}\"\n"
    "            else:\n"
    "                detail = f\"{_SOURCE_STATE_TEXT.get(state, state)} · {progress}%\"\n"
    "            if episode_text:\n"
    "                detail += f\" · {episode_text}\"\n"
    "            detail += f\"\\n（{self._source_trace(row)}）\"\n"
    "            cards.append({\n"
    "                \"component\": \"VAlert\",\n"
    "                \"props\": {\n"
    "                    \"type\": \"info\" if state in SOURCE_INFLIGHT_STATES else \"warning\",\n"
    "                    \"variant\": \"tonal\",\n"
    "                    \"density\": \"compact\",\n"
    "                    \"class\": \"mb-2\",\n"
    "                    \"title\": self._source_title(row),\n"
    "                    \"text\": detail,\n"
    "                },\n"
    "            })\n"
    "            if len(cards) >= 6:\n"
    "                break\n"
    "        return cards\n"
    "\n"
    "    def get_page(self):\n"
    "        \"\"\"返回单屏状态页，不再调用 super().get_page 拼接历史诊断卡。\"\"\"\n"
